| [`FINDING-20260815-a-consumer-is-not-a-file-extension.md`](FINDING-20260815-a-consumer-is-not-a-file-extension.md) | **`OI-81` CLOSED — and the classification that scoped its fix was wrong by an axis.** `BEN-325` split the guard's RED into 13 prose / 7 code **by file extension**; re-measured by OCCURRENCE, **only 2 of the 7 open the path** and 3 are pure docstrings — one being the P5A launcher, which reads the **annealed** arm and whose `fullevent_nominal/` line is a deliberate counter-example. Fixed with CLASS 6 (operand vs narrative, by COLUMN, failing closed), `EXIT 0` for the first time since the designation. **The hook's mechanical unlock trigger now fires and lane C declines to pull it: a trigger stops being mechanical once the check's CLAIM has been narrowed.** (`BEN-237`) |
| [`FINDING-20260815-a-failure-to-observe-rendered-as-an-observation.md`](FINDING-20260815-a-failure-to-observe-rendered-as-an-observation.md) | **`ACTIVE` was the ELSE branch of the Slurm classifier, so "could not look" rendered identically to "looked and saw running" — and `LIVE-STATE.md` showed Leg F ACTIVE for over 24 h after it COMPLETED, costing a fictional scheduling constraint on a ~39 GPU-h decision.** All three compute rows were unobserved; the host has no Slurm binaries at all, so that table was never evidence. The `observer_errors` proving it were computed and dropped at render. Fixed: `ACTIVE` now needs positive evidence. (BEN-323) |
| [`FINDING-20260815-a-guard-with-no-cell-for-what-it-cannot-see.md`](FINDING-20260815-a-guard-with-no-cell-for-what-it-cannot-see.md) | **`verify_hash_bindings.py` cannot see the Gate-5 implementation pins, and they are in NEITHER its `resolved` NOR its `unresolvable` count — the accounting has no cell for them, so its residue line makes the gap harder to find rather than easier.** 413 pairs harvested, 0 naming the dataloader or the replica-target builder, both pinned by active receipts. Historical coverage is unrecoverable: retro-fitting the 51 receipts is forbidden, not just costly. (BEN-322, OI-127) |
| `FINDING-20260815-a-citation-you-cannot-re-derive.md` | **`BEN-228`'s remedy assumes the citation is yours to update — and when the citing artifact is an emitted receipt or another lane's row, all three of its options are closed.** The fourth route: make the edit LINE-COUNT-NEUTRAL and verify it, so the coordinate stays true instead of being updated. Plus two near-misses the purpose-built gate caught and a careful reader did not — a non-covering pin grep read as absence of pins, and `| tail` converting a hard gate failure into `rc=0`; and `bash -n` cannot see broken Python inside a launcher heredoc, where the cost lands after the GPU is spent. (BEN-320) |
| `FINDING-20260817-a-crosscheck-that-measures-a-different-thing-than-its-name.md` | **A cross-check reported as ESTIMATOR DISAGREEMENT that identically measures a SUPPORT MISMATCH** — stage 6's `median 4.4% / max 72.9%` is `frac_seen - 1` to `1.19e-14`, the per-column unfolded-content ratio, so the cross-section conversion cancels exactly. **All three commutation mechanisms refuted by one table: the disagreement is LARGEST where only one W bin is reported, i.e. where no marginalization occurs.** Where the products cover the same content they agree to **0.45%**. (BEN-319) |
| `FINDING-20260816-share-what-you-are-not-testing.md` | **Redundancy that arises by construction is a FREE internal check, and trimming it is the tempting mistake** — two hooks in one loop duplicated `niter-1` rows, and gating them to exact equality turned *"the recorder might read at the wrong moment"* from a paragraph into a run that refuses. **And the harder rule, which resolves an apparent contradiction with `BEN-316`: a cross-check must SHARE exactly what it is not testing and DIFFER in exactly what it is.** "More independent" is not automatically stronger. (BEN-318) |
| `FINDING-20260816-the-arm-whose-instrumentation-nothing-pinned.md` | **The arm supplying `VL134`/`VL135`/`VL136` ran the 3-pin launcher, so nothing attests the instrumentation version it ran — and the old launcher printed no digests at all.** Recovered from the products' own schema instead (`253f25c0`), and the two arms of `VL138` ran **different** wrapper versions whose diff is one hunk entirely inside `if correct:`, so the contrast survives — measured, not assumed. **Still open: no receipt attests the anneal took effect**; the wrapper bypasses the module's own fail-closed assert and records a boolean that is `True` even on empty LR records. (BEN-317) |
| `FINDING-20260816-the-gate-that-measures-blas-blocking-noise.md` | **A projection gate whose "independent route" is the same product written one row at a time, so the only quantity it can measure is BLAS accumulation order — `1.9e-16` against a `1e-9` threshold, and EXACTLY `0.0` on the repo test's own fixture.** Confirms repair-10's `N3` by measurement, and corrects it twice: the verdict quotes the docstring's **disclaimer** as its promise, and "a check that cannot fail" is falsifiable and false — a `×2` edit to `project()` IS caught, while a corrupted `M` passes. `AGREED-WITH-CORRECTION`; the `BLOCK` is untouched. (BEN-316) |
| `FINDING-20260815-a-claim-about-code-read-from-its-structure.md` | **Four claims about code inferred from its structure, none read at the site, all four caught by another lane in one session** — a truncated `grep`, a dtype attributed to the wrong stream, a null called unanchored when it was definitional, and a row-vs-bin distinction that a sum destroys. `BEN-026` in non-shell clothing. **§6 adds a fifth, 2026-08-16: a `len(s) < 200` survey filter silently dropped 18 of 280 receipt leaves, so a documented field was called absent — a length filter over a document is a SEMANTIC filter, because the long leaves are the definitions.** (BEN-315) |
| `FINDING-20260815-a-suite-that-could-not-fail-on-its-own-interface.md` | **18 passing tests and 3 caught mutations could not see a dtype contract violation at the engine boundary** — arm 1 promoted float32 `weights_push` to float64 and job `57012031_3` died in the loss. The suite tested the arithmetic, never the collaborator it feeds. Remedy: call the engine's own loss with the array you produce. (BEN-314) |
| `FINDING-20260815-the-quarantine-measured-a-different-run.md` | **The quarantine manifest that rejects `VL100` on physics names the PRE-ANNEAL weights while its `job_id` is the ANNEALED closure — and the finalizer's own `hash:nominal-weights` check pins that same file, so the independent re-derivation confirms the defect instead of catching it. Lane D followed the manifest.** Also: the "four quotability grounds" was a count with no members; reconstructed there are three, two are one, and only `recovery_evaluated: False` survives — undeterminable read-only. (BEN-312, BEN-313; extends OI-125) |
| `FINDING-20260815-a-restatement-is-not-a-second-measurement.md` | **The closure certifying the annealed arm does not exercise the fold-forward deficit at all** (ratio `1.011418` vs the nominal's `0.736746`) — so it is SILENT about that failure mode, not reassuring; plus `VL100`'s falsification computed from the RETIRED arm's weights, and a per-cell field that is the unfolding's own output (Pearson `0.99973`) read as a contaminant of it. `VL100` unchanged at `0.512603276`; still clears under the well-posed correction. `OI-71` stays OPEN. (BEN-310, BEN-311, OI-125) |
| [`FINDING-20260815-a-designated-footing-restated-as-a-product.md`](FINDING-20260815-a-designated-footing-restated-as-a-product.md) | **A designated footing restated as a delivered measurement — and the contradiction was already three paragraphs above the insertion point.** `fps_provenance.PUBLICATION_BKG_MODE = "negweight-refined"` is real and fail-closed, and it is a footing **selected in code**; `unfolds_negweight_refined/` holds **ten `.config.json` and zero `.root`**, `PRODUCTION REMAINS GATED`, and `app_negweight.tex`'s own J22 paragraph already tells the note's reader the quoted number was not produced on that footing. **The runbook was NOT wrong** — *"the FPS lane already delivers the negweight-footed measurement"* is true of the analysis DESIGN, and false only once copied into a document that reads present tense as produced. **A designation and a product are the same words at different tenses, and the tense is what does not survive a relay.** Rule: before writing *"lane X carries/delivers Y"* into anything publication-bound, list the artifact — a constant is evidence of intent, only a file is evidence of a product. Cost of catching it: one `ls`. (BEN-350) |
| [`FINDING-20260815-a-clean-tree-that-had-already-pushed.md`](FINDING-20260815-a-clean-tree-that-had-already-pushed.md) | **A takeover session was told the predecessor "stopped responding" and the tree was clean. Both were true of the WORKTREE and false of the REMOTE** — the predecessor had finished and pushed, and the duplicate surfaced only at `git push` after a full passage, a two-pass three-PDF build and a commit. **`git status` answers "is my tree dirty", not "did the work already land"; for a lane resuming another lane's task those are different questions.** A dead channel is not an unfinished task — this predecessor was unreachable BECAUSE it had finished. `BEN-228` applied to liveness rather than indexes. **The duplication paid for itself:** the landed note text said *"neither producing launcher passes `--bkg-mode`"* in the present tense, which G-1 superseded — `run_p4_unfold_std.sh:111` passes it and `:120` stamps it, `p4_lib.py:225` pins it fail-closed — so the note **understated** the provenance guarantee. `BEN-350` mirrored: a plan read as a product there, a superseded code state read as current here. (BEN-351) |
| [`FINDING-20260815-true-when-written-then-copied-forward.md`](FINDING-20260815-true-when-written-then-copied-forward.md) | **A claim about code TRUE ON THE DAY IT WAS WRITTEN, fixed by a commit landing the SAME DAY, never rechecked, copied into four live documents and one of Joseph's rulings — where it became the sole premise of an "unfixable provenance regression" argument.** *"The launcher skips endpoints that already have a `bkg_mode` receipt"* described the PRE-`febb9a1` gate; today `run_p4_unfold_std.sh:77-84` skips **only on a PASSING `p4_check_receipt.py`**, and `bkg_mode` is required (`p4_lib.py:796-797`, enforced `:949-950`) and **compared** (`:961-962`) — so a receipt lacking it FAILS, is `rm -f`'d and re-runs. **The gate cast as the trap is the repair mechanism**; cost is compute, not irreversibility. Paired claim *"G-1 is code-only and not on the cluster checkout"* is **false on both halves**: cluster `HEAD` `683bdcc` contains `5a4009f`, and **stage 3 already ran there 2026-08-08** — ten ROOTs + ten `.done`, `mode=produced`, `bkg_mode=purity`, `code_rev=42268b6`. **Every copy was faithful; nobody misread anything** — `BEN-082` where the source was STRONG and merely DATED. Caught only because Joseph's grant required a non-author check, which returned **AGREED-WITH-CORRECTION** (agreeing the disposition, refuting the basis) — a verdict a plain agree/disagree bit cannot express. Two escalations NOT adjudicated: the run is unreconciled with the *"no cluster P4 run"* hold, and the products are untracked on purgeable scratch (`OI-75`). (BEN-352) |
| [`FINDING-20260815-an-instrument-recorded-the-neighbouring-quantity.md`](FINDING-20260815-an-instrument-recorded-the-neighbouring-quantity.md) | **An instrument built to close a "this quantity is not recorded" hole recorded the NEIGHBOURING quantity and made its own predeclaration look refuted by 75.8 draw-sd with the sign flipped.** The recorder hooks `RunStep1`, so the push `RunStep2(2)` leaves is consumed by nothing and recorded by no row — and that end-of-run one is what `OI-125` is about. Recovered, §2 AGREES at 1.17 prediction-sd. `BEN-360`. |
| [`FINDING-20260815-a-predeclared-pessimism-is-the-least-checked-claim.md`](FINDING-20260815-a-predeclared-pessimism-is-the-least-checked-claim.md) | **One conflation, two opposite-signed reading failures — and the quiet one would have been believed.** §6 declared arm 1 underpowered by sizing the perturbation off the end-of-run ratio while the correction is applied to the consumed one; realized amplitude was 4.04× declared and the effect 16.2× the pooled sd. A surpassed limitation triggers no investigation, which is why it needs its own id. `BEN-361`. |
| `FINDING-20260813-present-verdict-is-a-statement-about-the-search.md` | **A PRESENT verdict is also a statement about the search** — BEN-086's unstated half; four instances, two parties, where the source existed, was consulted, and the disqualifying content was in the output already on screen, so "check your sources" catches none of them (BEN-207) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-a-delegate-failure-has-no-reliable-signal.md`](FINDING-20260817-a-delegate-failure-has-no-reliable-signal.md) | **A delegate failure has no reliable signal — not the exit code, not the absence of a known error string.** `agy -p` exits **0** printing only a permission-denial notice (303 B of prose, so "non-empty" alone passes it); `codex exec` returned 0 on a usage-limit failure at 11:50Z and 1 on the same text at 13:05Z and 13:14Z, discriminator unknown. Three delegates word exhaustion three ways, so a predicate keyed to one phrase does not fire. Rule: **non-empty AND matches the required final-message format.** Executable: `delegate_report_check.py`. (BEN-390) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-a-narrowing-flag-guarantees-its-answer.md`](FINDING-20260817-a-narrowing-flag-guarantees-its-answer.md) | **A narrowing flag guaranteed the answer, and the narrowing is invisible in the result** — `--diff-filter=D` matches only commits that DELETE A FILE, so a method retired by editing was unfindable: 0 commits restricted vs **72** unrestricted, and the spec was at `a0cdc019`/`unified_throw_cov.py:224` with its derivation. Also: **N citations of one sentence is one source** (the second document quotes the first verbatim), and a claim about *documents* was read as settling a question about *code*. `BEN-235`'s second instance. (BEN-391) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-unqualified-transport-of-good-measurement.md`](FINDING-20260817-unqualified-transport-of-good-measurement.md) | **Four instances in one day, and no bad measurement among them** — each source was correct and its qualifier was dropped in transport (`7.8 GPU-h` at n=1 quoted for an n≥6 design; a documents-claim quoted about code; AI1's footing quoted for a 28.5× footing; a sha verified by `merge-base --is-ancestor`, which **every** sha on main passes — membership, never identity). Reader's rule: **re-derive the quoted number from its stated operands**, and run `git show --stat` beside any ancestry test. (BEN-392) |
| [`FINDING-20260818-p-measured-is-not-p-recorded-and-a-linkage-defect.md`](FINDING-20260818-p-measured-is-not-p-recorded-and-a-linkage-defect.md) | **`P` MEASURED is not `P` RECORDED — and a LINKAGE defect a timestamp fix cannot catch.** `CRITERIA` §2 cause 1's `P` names **X's RECEIPT** as the artifact that must carry the property; the census measured it and recorded it in **its own new receipt**, so `P_leg: MET` overstates the wording — **and the same author drew that exact distinction one leg over (`"M MEASURED is not M ACCEPTABLE"`) and did not carry it across the row.** **And the map cites the board twice while the board cites the map zero times: the information that would have prevented a four-hour dispatch existed, correct, ten hours before it.** `BEN-429` was a timestamp defect; **this is LINKAGE, and stating the map's `asOf` would not have caught it.** Directional test: if B cites A and A does not cite B, a reader of A cannot reach B. **Plus a third, intra-document instance — my own §2c heading. Row: `BEN-460`.** |
| [`FINDING-20260818-a-maps-currency-is-not-its-correctness.md`](FINDING-20260818-a-maps-currency-is-not-its-correctness.md) | **A routing document's CURRENCY is not its CORRECTNESS — a dispatch inherits its `asOf` and carries nothing that records it.** A 3-4 hour cause-1 job was dispatched off a map whose row was **accurate when written and stale by 2h40m**: the census script, its predeclaration and its committed receipt all exist, with `P_leg: MET` and `M_leg: MEASURED` and a positive control reproducing production's committed targets. **Distinct from `BEN-228` (wrong now) and `BEN-239` (right and unread): here the document was right AT ITS OWN TIMESTAMP, and the timestamp is the missing operand.** Rule, one field: a dispatch must state its source map's last-touched sha and time. **Row: `BEN-429`.** |
| [`DETERMINATION-20260818-lanec-clm012-status-conflates-two-claims.md`](DETERMINATION-20260818-lanec-clm012-status-conflates-two-claims.md) | **A ROW CAN STATE TWO CLAIMS OF DIFFERENT STRENGTH, and then no single status fits — `CLAIMS.md`'s vocabulary applies to ONE claim at a time.** `CLM-012`'s Gate 1 *reproduces the committed report* — **a replay, one route twice, which is `CLAIMS.md:4`'s own *"worker agreement is not verification"***; Gate 2 corroborates the NUMBERS against `BEN-038` and is genuinely independent. **But the CLAIM is an ATTRIBUTION — *most of the shortfall is specification rather than estimator quality* — inferred under a pre-registered rule, which removes the post-hoc degree of freedom and is NOT a second check.** **Determination: SPLIT. The numeric content keeps `VERIFIED-NUMERIC`; the attribution is `ASSUMED`.** Same move as splitting cause 3's `P` cell — two things with different remedies must not share a cell. **Row: `BEN-427`.** |
| [`RULING-20260817-lanec-unity-factor-diagnostic-is-admissible.md`](RULING-20260817-lanec-unity-factor-diagnostic-is-admissible.md) | **A VACUITY ARGUMENT IS ONLY AS COMPLETE AS ITS LIST OF DIFFERENCES — three of four held and the fourth carried the whole experiment.** *"At unity factors the replica must reproduce the nominal"*: `R` identical ✓, MC unthinned ✓, `imc` identical ✓ — **but the nominal CONSUMES the archived Gate-2 target while a replica REBUILDS it through the trained refinement**, so they differ by the measured `0.068%` refit residual plus process noise. The experiment asks whether `0.068%` produces the band's `186.1%` — an amplification of `2.7e3`, **and its failure mode is branch (a) MEASURED rather than nothing.** **Also: `(a)`-as-FACT was never refuted; only `(a)`-as-DISPOSITION was.** And a median of per-cell `z` over 84 correlated cells **is not a σ** — `4.6σ` withdrawn, `3.6σ` not adopted. **Row: `BEN-421`.** |
| [`RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-target-family-and-launchers.md`](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-target-family-and-launchers.md) | **A DEFAULT'S SAFETY IS A PROPERTY OF THE CALL GRAPH, NOT OF THE VALUE — *absence means three-stream* protected every existing launcher exactly as intended and became the silent failure the moment the other branch was authorized.** No launcher passed `--cstat-product` (`grep -c` → 0 across all three), so an `sbatch` would have spent **151 A100-h rebuilding the product that already exists**, passing every guard, distinguished only by a field nobody would read. **Ruled: disjoint output roots + a driver assertion that TAG ⟺ ROOT**, because a *required* flag would purchase edits to two PINNED launchers. **Plus: the data-only family needs its OWN target family** — the background fluctuation lives in the measured target, so reuse would make `P3` assert something false in the artifact. **Row: `BEN-420`.** |
| [`RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-normalization-must-vary.md`](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-normalization-must-vary.md) | **`normalize=True` DIVIDES OUT the fluctuation the weights carried, so one scalar becomes the ONLY route by which a resampled count reaches the answer — freeze it and the leading statistical term vanishes by exact cancellation, not by attenuation.** `omnifold/dataloader.py:149-153` sets `_c = normalization_factor/sumw`, so the measured block's total weight is exactly `1e6·R` whatever `w_refined` summed to. A frozen `R` makes `σ_stat^data` **shape-only** — the very defect `BEN-400` disqualified a ratio for this afternoon, now inside a number quoted to other experiments. **`R` must vary.** **And: assert BIT-EXACTNESS where the claim is that NOTHING HAPPENED; assert a TOLERANCED CLOSURE where the claim is that a COMPUTATION happened.** Bit-exactness on an arithmetic result checks the implementation path, not the claim. **Row: `BEN-408`.** |
| [`RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-coherence-predicate.md`](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-coherence-predicate.md) | **AN UNSATISFIABLE GUARD MAY BE CHECKING THE WRONG PROPOSITION RATHER THAN A TRUE ONE THE NEW PRODUCT FAILS — read what it asserts before deciding whether to skip it.** `validate_coherent_bootstrap` (`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:750`) is unsatisfiable by a data-only family: `:759` makes `sig_bootstrap_factor` **mandatory** while `:772` makes background **conditional**, and `:768` demands the persisted factor EQUAL the canonical draw — so omission raises, unity raises, and the real draw is `Route A`'s defect relocated into the receipt. **But `:768`'s content is *"the draw was not RE-DRAWN post-subset"* — it never checks the draw was APPLIED.** So the replacement asserts a **stronger** claim (no draw applied, verified on `w_truth` hashes) plus coherence **re-pointed at the data stream**: a RE-TARGETING, not a relaxation. Six predicates, differing per call site because `BEN-406`'s tense rule forbids extract re-deriving. **Row: `BEN-407`.** |
| [`FINDING-20260818-a-common-offset-forbids-pairs-not-values.md`](FINDING-20260818-a-common-offset-forbids-pairs-not-values.md) | **A common-offset scan's forbidden set is a condition on PAIRS of grid points, not a set of offset values — and the harm is aliasing BETWEEN members, not loss of structure within one.** `BEN-444`. Correction from the second party to `BEN-461`'s constraint: `{±958}` is the `k'=0` case, `k − k' == b2 − b1` is the condition, and a grid holding `100` and `1058` passes C's assertion and aliases. Caught before B wrote it. |
| [`FINDING-20260818-a-null-disjunct-is-silent-inside-a-nonempty-output.md`](FINDING-20260818-a-null-disjunct-is-silent-inside-a-nonempty-output.md) | **A disjunctive grep answers "did any of these appear", never "which" — and a null disjunct leaves no trace inside a non-empty result.** `BEN-443`. `split("|")`→0, `CLAIMS`→3, index→11, union→14: `3+11=14`, so the disjunct carrying the answer contributed nothing and was never seen. **The search was correct and complete; the union was read as an intersection.** Review lane's diagnosis, verified and filed here. |
| [`FINDING-20260818-the-word-owner-marks-the-absence-of-one.md`](FINDING-20260818-the-word-owner-marks-the-absence-of-one.md) | **In a column headed `lane/owner`, the word "owner" almost always marks the ABSENCE of one.** `BEN-442`. Trying to discharge `BEN-395`'s backlog by lifting owners out of row bodies yielded **1 of 62** — the rest say *"an owner to assign"*, *"an owner's call"*, *"nobody owns"*, or name a ROLE with no holder. Six rows name a **raiser**, and lifting those would have manufactured ownership. 62 → 40. |
| [`FINDING-20260818-both-endpoints-blind-in-the-same-direction.md`](FINDING-20260818-both-endpoints-blind-in-the-same-direction.md) | **Both endpoints of a peer channel are blind in the same direction, so their two accounts cannot converge — and a third reader can join the two logs on `msg_id`.** `BEN-440`. The census arrived in 21 s and was answered in 25 s, into terminal text no peer can read; the sender then inferred a channel defect from `success` plus silence. Join says **14/14 delivered** — defect affirmatively absent. Ships `peer_message_audit.py`, which raised three false "undelivered" of its own first. |
| [`FINDING-20260818-the-guard-cited-as-preventing-this-does-not-exist.md`](FINDING-20260818-the-guard-cited-as-preventing-this-does-not-exist.md) | **The row saying the free-list cannot go stale IS the stale row, and the guard it cites has never existed.** `BEN-441`. `test_findings_ben_blocks.py` is in no branch's history; the real check is in `whose_row.py`, is not in the hook, and is blind to this shape — measured PASS while `BEN-430`/`431` sit filed in the span still advertised as free. Ships `ben_filing_owner_check.py`. |
| [`FINDING-20260817-a-slurm-array-tally-needs-two-commands.md`](FINDING-20260817-a-slurm-array-tally-needs-two-commands.md) | **A SLURM ARRAY TALLY NEEDS TWO COMMANDS AND THE SUM IS THE CHECK.** `sacct -X` for terminal states, `squeue -r` for live ones, **and they must sum to the declared array size — either alone counts ROWS, not TASKS.** `30/1/9` reached Joseph for a family that was `10/4/36`. **Cause was NOT `-X` row counts** (that diagnosis was itself wrong): `--format=State%14` prints only State, so the `grep -v '.batch'` filter had no JobID to match and was a silent no-op — every task counted 3x. **Fourth register of the check that cannot fire: filtering on a field absent from the output.** **Row: `BEN-431`.** |
| [`FINDING-20260817-match-the-moment-and-the-null-survivor-list.md`](FINDING-20260817-match-the-moment-and-the-null-survivor-list.md) | **MATCH THE MOMENT, AND NEVER LET A NULL ON DEPENDENCE NAME A MECHANISM.** Rule 1: state which feature of the distribution the EVIDENCE constrains and which the CONCLUSION is about; if they differ the bridge must be argued. Rule 2: *"X does not depend on Y"* excludes **Y-driven** mechanisms only — **after any null, write the survivor list BEFORE the conclusion.** Draw-independence leaves nonlinear coupling AND a constant construction offset standing; one was named without the other being listed. **Three rules caught their own authors on the next inference in one day, and in every case the rule was applied to others' claims and not to the author's own — naming a failure mode raises the detection rate on INPUTS and leaves the GENERATION rate unchanged.** **Row: `BEN-430`.** |
| [`FINDING-20260817-two-pins-one-file-two-tenses.md`](FINDING-20260817-two-pins-one-file-two-tenses.md) | **Two pins on one file are a defect only if both claim the same TENSE — comparing a historical RECORD against a forward-looking GUARD manufactures a false positive that looks exactly like drift.** A floor launcher's `91144bee…` is a precondition (*"a floor is only a floor under the same code"*); an untracked runtime receipt's `66aa1f8f…` records what ran. Two moments, no disagreement. **`BEN-235` caught PROSPECTIVELY for the first time:** the covering grep named its own blind spot — adjacency form only, so it would miss `path`/`digest` in separate JSON fields — before the answer arrived, and was right, so the null was routed rather than recorded. **And `verify_hash_bindings.py` already knows the class**: `:370` compares current content, and `:78-80`'s `KNOWN_PREEXISTING` says rewriting a submit-time hash *"would falsify history"* — the limit is that its scope ends 2026-07-28 and the repo does not. **A binding mismatch is not automatically a thing to fix.** **Row: `BEN-406`.** |
| [`RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-cstat-is-a-second-product.md`](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-cstat-is-a-second-product.md) | **A data-only `C_stat` is a SECOND PRODUCT, not an amendment — and amending Gate 5 F7 would delete the only component carrying MC statistics.** Two arguments about two objects entail two objects: the budget has nowhere else to put MC statistics, AND *"statistical"* must mean what the field means (~88% of the three-stream counting-floor variance is MC, reducible by generating more MC). **The background stream is MC** (`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:568,:615,:1292` — POT-scaled negative injection), so data-only means **data only**, which settles 87% of the counting floor on what the background IS rather than on convention. `C_MCstat := C_stat^total − C_stat^data` is **not a covariance until PSD-checked** — the two families share one trained network, so the streams are not independent. **Rows: `BEN-404`.** |
| [`DETERMINATION-20260817-lanec-cstat-object-is-total-statistics.md`](DETERMINATION-20260817-lanec-cstat-object-is-total-statistics.md) | **`C_stat`'s object is TOTAL statistics — specified by Gate 5 F7, not chosen by the spec — and `OI-94`'s `90×` used a DATA-ONLY denominator against it.** `PET_UQ_REMEDIATION_STATUS.md:738-758` names signal-MC in three of six steps and also fixes the centring. **No `C_MCstat` component exists anywhere (0 occurrences), and `C_syst` universes vary physics parameters not sample size — so if `C_stat` did not carry it, nothing would.** Three-stream counting floor **`0.1426%`** (background-MC-dominated at `0.1331%`, `n_bkg = 564,591`) against the published data-only `0.0493%`: **`90.8×` → `31.4×`.** **Rows: `BEN-403` (the hypothesis defect), and this determination.** |
| [`RULING-20260817-lanec-oi126-branch-set-not-exhaustive.md`](RULING-20260817-lanec-oi126-branch-set-not-exhaustive.md) | **A REQUIRED difference between two arms reads as a discrepancy, and the natural remedy destroys the measurement — `OI-126`'s offered binary is NOT exhaustive.** `(a)` and `(b)` are both propositions about the family's DISPERSION; the whole of the evidence is about the nominal's LOCATION (median `z` `−0.128` → `+3.555` → `−1.828` across p‖, total at the 98th percentile). **And `(a)` is incoherent as a DISPOSITION regardless of its truth:** publishing the large bands as-is publishes a central value above the family's own MAXIMUM in 44 of 63 cells. The third branch is what the loader's own Gate-5 comment predicts — **the negweight refinement is INSIDE the bootstrap and is nonlinear**, so `E[refine(w)] ≠ refine(E[w])` and non-containment is expected. **Row: `BEN-402`.** |
| [`FINDING-20260817-comparable-on-one-axis-is-not-comparable.md`](FINDING-20260817-comparable-on-one-axis-is-not-comparable.md) | **A comparability check on one axis licenses nothing on another — and a validity inventory that listed a member VALID with zero of its eight criteria evaluated.** A probe whose docstring is TITLED `COMPARABILITY` matched the statistic (`rel_sd`, not `range/mean`) and never checked the QUANTITY: family from `["xsec"]` at `:21`, floor from `["central_vector"]` at `:30`, same `rsd` at `:38`. `central_vector` sums to 1, so the denominator understates and the inflated ratio biased toward the branch already being taken. **A named check narrows attention to its own axis.** And `gate6-floor-replication-result-56863958.json` has `draws` = `[2,3,4,5]` with 8 clauses each while `draw_1` carries no clauses — yet `inventory.draws_valid = [1,2,3,4,5]`; the missing field is `draws_checked`, and clause `8_execution_environment` is the axis the floor MEASURES. **Rows: `BEN-400`, `BEN-401`.** |
| [`FINDING-20260817-a-refusal-to-re-derive-is-not-an-absence.md`](FINDING-20260817-a-refusal-to-re-derive-is-not-an-absence.md) | **Evidence sitting unread behind a correctly-stated refusal to re-derive it — the INVERSE of every other cell on the quarantine board.** Cause 5 was graded `N/A — UNDECLARED` while `DETERMINATION-20260811-cause5-binding-half.md` §7 (`:233-237`) already answered it; the grade was taken partly *because* a routing map said *"I did not re-derive it."* **`CLAUDE.md`'s own write-once convention makes that sentence what a CORRECT lane emits constantly**, so it carries zero information about whether the answer exists — the failure is a cost of the convention, not a deviation from it. **The rule: *"no document answers this"* is a claim about the CORPUS and needs a covering search; *"no document I read answers this"* is a claim about the READER.** Distinct from `BEN-235`/`BEN-391`, where a search was run and could not have refuted its conclusion — here none was run. Carries the `OI-6`→`OI-3` citation rider and lane B's owner-weight caveat. **Row: `BEN-239` (exhausts lane C's block).** |
| [`FINDING-20260817-a-correction-site-inherits-the-index-trust.md`](FINDING-20260817-a-correction-site-inherits-the-index-trust.md) | **The mechanism added to stop a dead number propagating was propagating its successor's scoping defect** — the supersession index's *what supersedes it* column said `39.078` bare, which is the GPU column only, from the file whose own header says read it AT WRITE TIME. **A supersession entry is written by whoever is most convinced the old value is wrong, satisfies its reader's diligence step, and is checked by nothing.** `BEN-302`'s complement: that row is about a retraction's REACH, this is about the quality of what arrives. (BEN-393) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-a-trigger-is-a-stored-measurement.md`](FINDING-20260817-a-trigger-is-a-stored-measurement.md) | **An unlock trigger written to remove judgement is a stored measurement, and the commit that satisfies it can be the commit that breaks it.** The dispatcher's *"no judgement call — run the command"* trigger was reported fired; measured `PASS/0` at `8a34f12^` and `FAIL/1` at HEAD, on two files `8a34f12` itself added — **true when measured, false when sent, by its author's own commit.** Plus a second, independent reason it must not be wired that survives a green (`BEN-387`). NOT wired; routed to Joseph. (BEN-394) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-a-lane-owner-column-that-names-no-lane.md`](FINDING-20260817-a-lane-owner-column-that-names-no-lane.md) | **A column named `lane/owner` whose cells are mostly not owners — 65 of 93 `OI-*` rows name an AREA, so 70 % of the table cannot be routed from its own row.** `OI-58` routes its hop-1 fix to nobody; a populated cell READS as ownership, which is `BEN-247`'s mechanism in the control plane. The vocabulary for lane / `unowned` / subject-vs-filer already exists and is unapplied. Report shipped, deliberately not a gate. (BEN-395) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-verification-allocated-by-suspicion.md`](FINDING-20260817-verification-allocated-by-suspicion.md) | **Verification effort is allocated by suspicion, and suspicion tracks DISAGREEMENT rather than FRAGILITY** — every defect caught today was a claim someone doubted; the ones MISSED arrived as SUPPORT, which is more dangerous because nobody downstream has a reason to check a citation doing rhetorical work. **And naming the bias does not fix it: the lane that named the species committed it one turn later.** Corrective: resolve every citation you repeat, and state your own position's cost the way an opponent would. (BEN-396) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-the-reconciliation-was-in-the-receipt.md`](FINDING-20260817-the-reconciliation-was-in-the-receipt.md) | **A declared `N`, a submitted array range and a product count are three quantities, and the RECEIPT is the authority that reconciles them.** `56863958` declares `N=5` with 4 payloads; two lanes read a lost draw, a third read *"never submitted, sample is 4"* — and the receipt says `array_tasks: "2-5"` with `draw_1` an **EXISTING artifact reused, NOT retrained**, value recorded. **`N=5` = 1 reused + 4 new; the sample is 5, and `n=4` would have inflated the floor the `OI-126` reading depends on.** (BEN-397) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-two-sessions-one-checkout-and-no-signal.md`](FINDING-20260817-two-sessions-one-checkout-and-no-signal.md) | **Two sessions committed from the main checkout all day and NOTHING signalled it** — every other lane had its own worktree. Measured near-miss: one session's `FINDINGS.md` copy predated another's commit, so committing it would have **silently deleted lane B's `BEN-248` row and its index entry, as a clean-looking commit with nine passing hook checks.** Fix: assert `--git-dir != --git-common-dir` before committing. **`BEN-330`'s prior instance finally gets its cause.** (BEN-398) |
| [`FINDING-20260817-default-identity-is-the-machine-owner.md`](FINDING-20260817-default-identity-is-the-machine-owner.md) | **Unattributed agent work is not anonymous — it is attributed to the human who owns the machine.** 61 of ~109 commits in 10 h authored `Joseph Bailey`, none his, **including all thirteen of mine — one of which says `%an` is not a lane field.** A false positive that looks like legitimate authorship, biased in one direction, defeating every ownership audit. Fallback now `…agent (unattributed)@…invalid`; lanes pass `-c` per commit. (BEN-399) |

The mirror case (e±, where they match Rodrigues and we do not) sits 40 lines later and §5 calls our own divergence *"a declared convention choice."* Substance defensible either way; **the asymmetry is the first thing an advisor sees, and it discredits numbers that are sound.** **Check outbound documents for who gets which verb.** [full](FINDING-20260813-the-summary-that-outran-its-sources.md) **Repaired at `4788598`**, which puts the e± mirror adjacent to the defect sentence; the verb stands but is no longer met alone. Both errors make the repair look smaller and more greppable than it is, so "or nothing" produces the partial change it forbids. [Probe](state/probe-eavail-pion-mass-sites-20260813.py) [full](FINDING-20260813-the-summary-that-outran-its-sources.md) *Session D.* | — | EP-2026-08-13-eavail-audit |
| BEN-180 | **A band check tested only on the side the data is not on.** Gate-6 Leg F's `d_by_draw = abs(v-1.0)` is correct; **dropping the `abs` survives all 52 acceptance tests.** All four band tests use draws at or above 1.0 — three via `stats_at`, which hand-builds the stats dict and never calls `floor_statistics`, so the `abs` is not on their path. Without it every draw BELOW 1 is unconditionally in-band, and members 4/5 sit at `0.8198`/`0.7535` while draw 3's iter-0 is `0.8400` — **the below-1 half is where this campaign's data lives.** `BEN-173`'s shape: a control present for one sibling, absent for its mirror. [Probe](state/probe-gate6-floor-mutation-20260813.py) [full](FINDING-20260813-tested-on-the-side-the-data-is-not-on.md) *Session D.* | — | EP-2026-08-13-gate6-floor |
| BEN-183 | **The repository I measured was not the repository I reported on.** I ran `verify_hash_bindings.py` in a lane worktree, got two MISMATCHes, and built a design review's decisive evidence on them — then merged `origin/main` and pushed. `git merge-base --is-ancestor` returns NO for both repairs against the commit I measured at; on `main` the gate reads **ALL BINDINGS INTACT**. **A lane worktree is a different tree from `main` and says so in `git status` only if you look.** State the ref you measured at, in the sentence that carries the number — `d09caf1` and `main` are not interchangeable, and a verdict that turns on a measurement inherits its scope silently. [full](FINDING-20260813-the-tree-i-measured-was-not-the-tree-i-reported-on.md) *Session D, against itself.* | — | EP-2026-08-13-hook-review |
| BEN-452 | **A probe that forces a guard FALSE cannot test that guard — and mutation review cannot rescue it, because mutating an unreachable branch changes no observable.** The `M(ii)` launcher probe stubs `python3`/`sbatch` in a temp dir, so **no product exists and the resume guard is false on every run**. Its assertion is on the **observed argv**, which only exists on the branch where the guard is false — so the skip branch is unobservable **in principle**, and that branch is where the defect lives: all 50 offsets write fixed-literal paths already complete on NERSC, so a real launch **resume-skips, exits 0 without running Python, and returns 50 copies of the published archive.** Five lanes missed it, two by mutation review. **Delete the resume guard and every probe assertion still passes; invert it and they still pass.** Checks: *what does my setup make FALSE?* and *is my assertion downstream of a guard?* [full](FINDING-20260818-a-probe-that-forces-a-guard-false-cannot-test-it.md) *Session D, on the Codex session's find.* | `BEN-258`, `BEN-450`, `BEN-255` | EP-2026-08-17-cause4 |
| BEN-451 | **A search for the token you ALREADY HOLD is the cheapest one available — and the one most often skipped, because holding it feels like having used it.** Chasing `BEN-259` I walked the dependency chain (`git ls-files`, two hops), then a CFS `find` (killed, then timed out at 90 s), then hashed 9.9 GB. Lane A then found the answer in **one `git grep`**: `state/restore-step1-g2-durability-20260804.json`, committed, `PASS`, two weeks old, **naming both paths**. **I had the CFS path in hand and searched the filesystem it pointed into rather than the repository I was standing in.** Why it works: the repo indexes by CONTENT, and a preservation receipt under `state/` is on no dependency path from the artifact that depends on it — no walk reaches it, only a content search, and the content is the token. Prompt, asked whenever a search returns empty or expensive: **what am I already holding, and have I grepped for it?** Four sibling query defects (wrong predicate / wrong spelling / predicate changed mid-search / corpus unstated) are wrong QUESTIONS; **this one is a question never asked.** [full](FINDING-20260818-search-for-the-token-you-already-hold.md) *Session D; lane A found the receipt and insisted this be a rule rather than a confession.* | `BEN-255`, `BEN-259` | EP-2026-08-17-cause4 |
| BEN-450 | **Detection without propagation is a checked box, not a barrier — and the test could not tell the difference.** `eavailW_covariance.py`'s cause-6 coverage guard promises three verbs, *count it, name it, and PUT IT IN THE OUTPUT*; the value is computed at `:349`, printed, and **never referenced again**, while `:492-508` writes histograms only. So the artifact still carries unsupported bins that read as infinitely precise, and the only channel is a `print` on a filesystem where `BEN-028` records 4 MiB stdout block-buffering. **The test asserts the value is COMPUTED while its own failure message says *"bound to a name and reported"* — delete both prints, leave it computed and unused, and every test passes.** *A positive control on a static test controls only the static property.* Operational half: **a detect-only guard makes the rebuild that consumes it a waste, so the repair must precede the rebuild.** Rules: name the consumer; write the negative case unconditionally (`unified_throw_cov.py:482-489`, same quarantine, same date — *absence is indistinguishable from zero*); test propagation with the mutation that kills it. [full](FINDING-20260818-detection-without-propagation.md) *Session D, on the mediator's cause-6 dispatch.* | `BEN-250`, `BEN-255`, `BEN-258`, `BEN-259`, `BEN-028` | EP-2026-08-17-cause4 |
| BEN-259 | **"Reproducible" is not "recoverable" — and the receipt is tracked at EVERY HOP, so the search stops before it sees the payload is missing.** `powered_closure/.gitignore` excludes the multi-GB artifact as *"reproducible from [the receipts] plus the dump"* — **true, and it does not say the dump is itself untracked (root `.gitignore:29`), 9.9 GB, and on the same PURGEABLE `/pscratch`.** The guarantee **relocates** the risk to a second object under identical exposure. `CLM-012`'s re-verification opens both (`d2_acceptance_oracle.py:94` ART, `:99` DUMP). **Why it is a class:** `git ls-files` at both hops returns receipts and no payload, so `OI-130`'s *"is the backing artifact tracked?"* finds a receipt at the artifact, follows the promise to the dump, and finds a receipt there too — **each link individually looks discharged.** Rule: **follow the promise, not the object** — enumerate what the reproduction CONSUMES, recursively, terminating only at something tracked, off-scratch, or a stated cost. Remedy: a reproducibility promise must state its preconditions' storage class. **My own retraction is load-bearing: I said "unrepeatable"; it is reproducible via a GPU re-run — increasingly expensive on a clock nobody watches, not lost.** [full](FINDING-20260818-reproducible-is-not-recoverable.md) **RESOLVED 2026-08-18: the copy EXISTS and is byte-identical** — `/global/cfs/cdirs/m3246/josephrb/minerva-shutdown-stage/g2_input/`, `sha256 = fa6b3463…` **= the pin itself**, CFS not purgeable, receipt staged beside it. So the branch is **backed but undocumented** and the remedy shrinks to *one clause naming the path*. **The finding is unchanged** — it was never *the dump might be purged*, it was *the promise does not say what it depends on* — and **the chain result stands: a CFS copy nobody wrote down is not discoverable by asking "is the backing artifact tracked?". Preserved and findable are different properties and only the first is established.** **Amendment 1 (lane A): I was WRONG that it is unindexed** — `state/restore-step1-g2-durability-20260804.json` is committed, `PASS`, dated 2026-08-04, and names both paths; 8 tracked files mention the directory. **I had the CFS path in hand and never grepped the repo for it** — the cheapest search available is the one I did not run. So the branch is **BACKED AND DOCUMENTED**, and **the surviving defect is sharper: the `.gitignore` and the durability receipt reference each other ZERO times in either direction — the condition IS stated and is unfindable from the artifact that depends on it.** Remedy shrinks to *cross-reference the receipt*, and `OI-130` can index `state/restore-*-durability-*.json` instead of walking CFS. My first search was KILLED and is reported as killed; the bounded re-run exited 124, so the existential holds and no universal is claimed. *Session D; the chain and the local confirmations are lane A's.* | `BEN-255`, `BEN-258`, `OI-130` | EP-2026-08-17-cause4 |
| BEN-258 | **`cannot-fail` is a two-place predicate — check x input DOMAIN — and deleting a vacuous guard can delete a spec.** `submit_gate5_data_only_n50.sh:46` and **`:47`** both compare `$DATA_ROOT` plus a fixed differing suffix, so neither can fail — **two vacuous guards, not one**, and `:47` is the line proposed as *"the one doing real work"* when only `:46` had been found. **They are vacuous because `:20` is a LITERAL — a fact about `:20`, not about the guards.** Making it overridable (the rebuild's route) CREATES a real failure mode — a root path containing a family-root component, passing both and caught only by `L2`'s `clash` in Python at the builder — so the repair is not deletion but **the guard the change makes checkable.** Detection question, asked only of checks already found vacuous: *what would have to become variable for this to fire?* If the answer names a change already proposed, **it is a specification, not dead code.** Refines `BEN-250` by making its domain explicit; does NOT revise `BEN-256` amendment 1, whose second reason survives any widening. [full](FINDING-20260818-cannot-fail-is-a-two-place-predicate.md) **Amendment 1 — a THIRD category with a DISTINCT remedy, which is the test of a real one:** *can fire, never has* -> **unverified; do not TRUST it, exercise it.** `submit_...n50.sh:39`'s `! -L` was live all along (a fixed path can still be a symlink), so E's *"non-vacuous over the domain my change introduced"* was flattering and untrue — **and so was my own "the override gives those guards something to check", which is right for `:46`/`:47` and wrong for `:39`. Two guards, one file, opposite categories.** *Session D; `:46` and the launcher repair are lane E's, as is the `:39` instance; mediator confirmed both halves.* | `BEN-250`, `BEN-255`, `BEN-256` | EP-2026-08-17-cause4 |
| BEN-257 | **A lane that answers a census in terminal text is indistinguishable, at the asker, from one that is capped — and the failure is silent at BOTH ends.** A answered and made **zero** `SendMessage` calls; the tool's contract is that plain text *is not visible to other agents*. **No delivery receipt, no sender-side error**, so the cost falls entirely on the party who cannot detect it, and the census carried a lane state inferred from silence. Rule: an answer is not an answer until it is a `SendMessage`, and an asker must write `NO RESPONSE` rather than a status inferred from it — `BEN-027` applied to lane states, not only job ids. | BEN-229 | **`sacct` UNDER-REPORTED A LIVE ARRAY BY TWO TASKS, AND `BEN-210`'s PRESCRIBED REMEDY RETURNS EMPTY ON A PENDING ARRAY.** In one 2-second window on `56993778`: `squeue -r` → five tasks `_1.._5` all PENDING; `sacct -X` (and `-X -D`) → `56993778_[3-5]`, **three**. Also worse than `BEN-210` in two ways: **three** tasks alias at once, and **the aliasing set SHRINKS as tasks are split**, so a cached mapping silently over-reports health. **Checks: `sacct` is NOT authoritative for an array that has not started — use `squeue -r` for pending state and `scontrol show job <array>_<task>` for the raw id; re-resolve rather than cache; and name the TOOL beside any count, because `BEN-027`'s same-turn rule is not sufficient when two tools disagree in the same second.** Lane A's own Leg-0 report said `[1-5]` and was right — by luck of tool choice, and a verifier using `sacct` alone would have called it mis-stated. | BEN-157 | **The tool whose only job is to refuse a partial family passes on zero members — and six sibling checks trust a claim instead of measuring the world.** `:528` `--n type=int` with no floor: `--n 0` on an empty dir returns rc=0 and the exact `FAMILY_COMPLETE_PASS`, and a real 3-member family passes at `--n 3` while `PARTIAL` at `--n 50` **with the artifacts unchanged**. Training `PRESENT` is receipt-only, and **`completion_marker_valid` is never read anywhere in the tool** — the receipt asserts its own marker validity and nothing checks it. The `NAME_MISMATCH` stray scan is reachable only when the receipt is *absent*, so it catches a file disagreeing with the launcher but **not a receipt agreeing with a wrong file**. The `.done` check compares `output`+`size` but not `mtime`, so the tool accepts what `atomic_write.is_complete` rejects — and **no receipt is ever hashed against anything**, while `is_complete`'s `mtime` is second-resolution so mirroring it is a floor. Null `R` yields 43-of-43 clean and `R_recorded: null` — **four checks vanished with nothing reporting it**, although `--skip-replay` already implements the correct downgrade fifteen lines away. And `test_expected_names_match_the_launcher` **never opens the launcher**; it asserts against duplicated literals, which I had described to a peer as pinned to the launcher — withdrawn. **73 tests could not have found any of it: they are written in the tool's own idiom** (`n=2`/`n=3` fixtures, never a fixed 50). No emitted verdict is invalidated — every run used the default and returned `PARTIAL` — but promotion is BLOCKED and accepted as blocked: at 50/50 the pass is unfalsifiable, not wrong. What was wrong is that nothing on disk said so while `NOTE.md` invited the comparison. **No digit of any digest was changed** — the ledger's *quantity* cell now names which receipt, which commit, and that no file on disk carries it. | BEN-231 | **A mask that is drawn per replica turns mask flicker into variance, and no available check can see it.** *(Filed when the design had two blind builders, whose perfect agreement was the point; Joseph dropped the second builder hours later, which removes the agreement check entirely and makes the hazard STRONGER, not weaker — the spec declaration is now the only protection. So the **reporting domain is itself a draw**, and a thinly-populated cell can be reported in one member and hard zero in another. **Mechanism found by D; occurrence measured by C on the 14 published members, and it OCCURS: 3 cells reported in some but not all — one in only 9 of 14** — with the artifacts' own `n_cells_populated` telemetry varying `260/261/262`. In such a cell a third of the across-replica spread is the mask switching off, **not fluctuation of the cross section**, and the resulting variance is wrong. **The reason this is a finding and not a bug report is the failure mode of the fix:** the campaign's defence here was two independent blind builders compared element-wise, and **both builders, given identical member vectors, would compute the identical wrong variance and agree to the last bit.** Independence buys nothing against a defect in the *input* to both. **The design then went to ONE builder, so even that check is gone** — which is the generalisable half: the hazard was never sensitive to how many builders there were, because it lives upstream of all of them. | BEN-233 | **`sacct` prints local time and `date -u` does not, and the difference is exactly the offset you are trying to measure.** Diagnosing extraction array `56936015`, I asked for `date -u` (11:56Z) and read `sacct` `End` timestamps (last completion `04:56:39`) as if both were UTC. That reads as **7 hours with no completions** — a throughput collapse — and I was one step from reporting it to the mediator, who would have taken it to Joseph as a stalled array. It was caught because **the gap was suspiciously round**: 7 h is exactly `UTC-0700`, and a real collapse has no reason to land on the hour. Verified with `SLURM_TIME_FORMAT='%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S UTC%z'`, which prints the offset explicitly: task 13 ended `04:56:39 PDT`, **two minutes before I looked.** Nothing was wrong. The same turn also caught a second inference-for-measurement: **the array's throttle was reported to me as 2 and is actually 10** (`ArrayTaskThrottle=10`) — two concurrent tasks was *observed occupancy* under `(Priority)`, not a configured cap, so the proposed fix of raising the cap would have bought nothing and the real constraint is queue priority. Both halves are the same error: **a number that was inferred from a symptom rather than read from the thing that sets it.** **GENERALISED 2026-08-14 after two more instances, one mine and one the mediator's, both the same day — and the original mitigation would have caught neither.** *Print the offset* is right for `sacct` and useless for `git`: **`TZ=UTC git log --date=format:` does NOT apply `TZ`** (only `format-local:` does) and **`format:` STRIPS the offset** unless the format string asks for `%z`, so the round-number tell my own case relied on is **absent**. The general remedy is **establish the frame before comparing two timestamps, and prefer a representation that cannot omit the frame** — `%cI`/`%ct` for git, epoch or an explicit offset for `sacct`. Before grading a cause `UNDECLARED`, open the document the index already points at and read it AGAINST THE QUESTION.** Related but distinct from `BEN-235`/`BEN-391`, where a search was run and could not have refuted the claim drawn from it — **here no search was run at all, and a refusal to summarise was read as the absence of a verdict.** **RIDER, same class, committed in the paragraph recording it:** C's §4 cited the corroboration as *"`OPEN_ITEMS` item 6"*, which does not resolve — `OI-6` is the standard-P4 purity decision (`:74`); the claim's live home is `OI-3` (`:71`) with its original phrasing **archived** at `OPEN_ITEMS-ARCHIVE-2026-08.md:834`, and `grep -ciE 'transferab' docs/OPEN_ITEMS.md` → **`0`**. Found by lane B while landing the declaration, **recorded as failed rather than silently repaired**, which is the only reason it reached the party repeating it. **AND B'S WEIGHT CAVEAT, WHICH PROTECTS THE GRADE RATHER THAN WEAKENING IT: `OI-3`'s owner cell reads `PET / cause 5 owner`, so that corroboration is the OWNING LANE'S OWN and cannot be the second outside voice the `UNDECLARED` grade was asking for. | BEN-444 | **A COMMON-OFFSET SCAN'S FORBIDDEN SET IS A CONDITION ON *PAIRS* OF GRID POINTS, NOT A SET OF OFFSET VALUES — AND THE HARM IS ALIASING *BETWEEN* MEMBERS, NOT LOSS OF STRUCTURE *WITHIN* ONE.** Correction from the second party to a constraint lane C derived in `BEN-461`'s determination; **C is right that the constraint exists and belongs in a launcher assertion, and right on the non-obvious half — a common offset can NEVER merge the groups, `b1+k == b2+k ⟺ b1 == b2`.** Two coherence groups by estimator-seed baseline (lane B's measurement): `g1` = `sweep_bank_5d`/`bootstrap_nd`/`seedscan_split` at **42**, `g2` = `unified_throw_cov`'s throws+block+CV at **1000**. **DEFECT 1 — `{±958}` IS THE `k'=0` SPECIAL CASE.** The collision condition holds between two grid points: `b1 + k == b2 + k' ⟺ k − k' == b2 − b1 == 958`. Verified counterexamples, none caught: `g1@1058 = g2@100 = 1100`; `g1@500 = g2@-458 = 542`; **grid `[0,100,500,958,1058,1500]` has colliding pairs `(0,958)` AND `(100,1058)`, and C's assertion flags only the first.** **CORRECT FORM: no two scanned offsets may differ by any `b_i − b_j`** — still one line, over pairs rather than values. **`assert k not in (958,-958)` IS WORSE THAN NO ASSERTION: it lets the aliasing through WITH A GUARD'S ENDORSEMENT** — `BEN-405`'s shape inside the guard written against `BEN-405`'s shape, which is `BEN-333` one level in. **DEFECT 2 — THE HARM IS THE WRONG DEFECT, AND A WRONG HARM AIMS THE DEBUGGING WRONG.** The determination says at `k=±958` *"the structure the offset exists to preserve is destroyed"*. **Measured: `g1 → 1000`, `g2 → 1958`, DISTINCT — the within-run co-variation structure is INTACT.** What happens is that `g1@958` uses the seed `g2@0` used, so two ENSEMBLE MEMBERS share a realisation across groups: **spurious aliasing between grid points, which biases an `M(ii)` spread DOWNWARD** — quieter than a broken structure, surfacing as a too-small uncertainty rather than as anything wrong. The assertion's message must name aliasing between grid points, or the next reader hunts a corrupted structure and finds none. **DEFECT 3 — THE CONSTRAINT'S NECESSITY RESTS ON A PREMISE THE SAME DOCUMENT DECLINES TO RELY ON.** Both C's set and this row's are needed **only if a shared seed VALUE across DIFFERENT legs actually correlates the noise** — recorded in that determination's own lineage as `CONSIDERED-AND-DECLINED`: *"perhaps the perturbations decorrelate. That is an empirical claim nobody has measured."* **If the premise holds, C's set is under-inclusive; if it fails, the constraint is unnecessary — either way the determination treats as structural certainty a claim it elsewhere holds open**, and it was relayed onward as fact. **Impose it anyway on conservatism — excluding pairs is free and the failure is silent — but state it as CONDITIONAL on the unmeasured premise: the arithmetic says WHICH offsets collide; only the premise makes a collision harmful.** **NOTHING REOPENED:** the `(i)`/`(ii)` ruling is C's, seconded here, and C's derivation from the retired jitter term is a derivation where this lane's *do not let measurability choose the specification* was a heuristic. **No scan has run; no launcher was written or edited; the determination is not edited and should carry a POINTER to this row rather than absorb it.** *Caught before lane B wrote the assertion — B was stopped mid-task.* [full](FINDING-20260818-a-common-offset-forbids-pairs-not-values.md) *Seconding lane. Baselines and the group map are lane B's measurement, not re-derived here.* | `BEN-461`, `BEN-405`, `BEN-333`, `BEN-443` | EP-2026-08-18-gbdt-note-blockers |
| BEN-443 | **A DISJUNCTIVE GREP ANSWERS "DID ANY OF THESE APPEAR", NEVER "WHICH" — AND A NULL DISJUNCT IS INVISIBLE INSIDE A NON-EMPTY OUTPUT.** One command, three unrelated patterns, asking *does anything parse `CLAIMS.md` by column index?* — `grep -nE "split\(.\|.\)|CLAIMS|\[[0-9]+\]"`. The `parts[0..2]` lines matched the INDEX disjunct; the `CLAIMS.md` strings at `whose_row.py:381-383` matched the CLAIMS disjunct. **They are unrelated lines — those strings are TEST FIXTURES where the filename is a VALUE in a tab-separated `ROW-OWNERS.tsv` row — and the union was read as an INTERSECTION.** **MEASURED, disjunct by disjunct: `split("|")` → 0 (THE ANSWER), `CLAIMS` → 3, `\[[0-9]+\]` → 11, union → 14. `3 + 11 = 14`, so the null disjunct contributes no line, no blank, no marker — and the output looked full because the others fired.** **ITS OWN REGISTER WITHIN `BEN-389`'s FAMILY, and the difference is the point: in every prior instance the SEARCH was wrong — wrong scope, wrong operating point, wrong matching semantics, a filter on an absent column. HERE THE SEARCH WAS CORRECT, COMPLETE, AND RETURNED TRUE INFORMATION; every printed line was a real match. The entire defect is in reading a union as a conjunction, so *"check your grep"* fails — the grep was fine.** **THE OVERLAP IS THE LOAD-BEARING CHECK AND IT IS ZERO — without it the equation proves nothing**, since `3 + 11 = 14` is equally consistent with 4 CLAIMS and 12 index lines sharing 2. Measured: CLAIMS on lines `381 382 383`, index on `209 227 272 279 282 298 531 534 652 697 698`, **intersection empty**, so the union is the exact DISJOINT sum and the null's invisibility is PROVED rather than suggested. Supplied by the review lane against its own finding after this row was filed. **REMEDY: run the disjuncts SEPARATELY whenever the conclusion depends on WHICH fired.** **AND ROBUST TURNS OUT TO BE CHEAP: `awk '/p1/{a++} /p2/{b++} /p3/{c++} END{printf "%d %d %d\n", a+0, b+0, c+0}'` emits `0 3 11` — ONE command with an explicit zero, nothing absent to notice, because `+0` forces the unset counter to coerce.** **AND ITS NAIVE FORM REPRODUCES THIS ROW'S DEFECT ONE LAYER DOWN:** the same awk with `%s` prints `[] [3] [11]`, because an unset awk variable renders EMPTY — **the tool proposed to make the null visible hides it again unless you force the coercion.** Generalised: **write any multi-pattern query so a non-matching pattern EMITS A VALUE rather than nothing.** The `grep -oE … | uniq -c` tally is demoted to *acceptable when you only need whether, not which*: it shows `split("|")` **absent** rather than zero, and noticing a missing fifth row is the same act that failed the first time. **THE TRANSFERABLE HABIT, the review lane's and narrower than the credit it declined: it cited `whose_row.py:209` and `:697-698` rather than writing "the parser reads only the first three columns" — A LINE NUMBER IS A FALSIFIABLE CLAIM; A DESCRIPTION OF WHAT CODE DOES IS NOT.** A reader can open the file and disagree with a line number; a description invites agreement. `BEN-380` from the writer's side. **THE CONSEQUENCE FOR THE CASE THAT PRODUCED IT:** `CLM-006` carries **13 fields between its pipes against the header's 9** (`line.split("|")` gives 15 vs 11 by counting the empties outside the outer pipes — **the between-pipes base is correct and this lane reported the other without stating its convention**), so its `independent verifier` field reads *"median 1.70% (prior-dominated cells stay near prior…)"*. **NOTHING MISREADS IT BECAUSE NOTHING READS IT — verified: there is NO column-indexed consumer of `CLAIMS.md` anywhere**, every `split("|")` hit checked individually. **That INVERTS the usual argument for a check: a per-row field-count assertion is not hardening a blind consumer, it is the ONLY thing that would ever notice**, since the file's correctness is maintained by nobody parsing it and this surfaced only because a human counted fields for an unrelated purpose — and on a file with zero parsers it carries **no regression risk**, which is usually the objection. **NOT REPAIRED HERE:** `CLAIMS.md` is lane C's. [full](FINDING-20260818-a-null-disjunct-is-silent-inside-a-nonempty-output.md) *Mechanism, diagnosis and formulation are the review/second-key lane's (`Assistant [28640e]`), self-reported within one turn and filed here at its request because it is read-only. **What this lane contributed is the check that produced it: the review lane's CONCLUSION was right and its PREMISE was wrong** — `whose_row.py:209`/`:697-698` split on `\t` and read `OWNERS_TSV`, not `CLAIMS.md`. **A right answer suppresses scrutiny of its reasoning** (`BEN-396`), and a supporting premise inside a correct conclusion is the least-scrutinised position there is.* | `BEN-389`, `BEN-396`, `BEN-391` | EP-2026-08-18-oi-ownership |
| BEN-442 | **IN A COLUMN HEADED `lane/owner`, THE WORD "OWNER" ALMOST ALWAYS MARKS THE ABSENCE OF ONE — AND THE REPAIR ANYONE WOULD ATTEMPT FIRST MAKES IT WORSE IN EXACTLY THE ROWS THAT LOOK EASIEST.** Extends `BEN-395`. Plan was mechanical: lift the party each row already names into the column and cite the sentence. **IT YIELDED 1 ROW OF 62** — `OI-47`, *"re-scoped 2026-08-12, owner Session A"*. **THE OTHER OCCURRENCES FAIL IN TWO WAYS. (1) THE WORD IS USED TO SAY OWNERSHIP IS UNASSIGNED:** `OI-22` *"Scoped work with an owner to assign"*; `OI-41` *"whichever the owner prefers"* / *"is the owner's call"*; `OI-60` *"an owner's call and is NOT taken here"*; `OI-70` *"left for whoever owns that file"*; `OI-73` *"editable-by-whoever-owns-it"*; `OI-64`(C) *"nobody owns re-deploying those"* and *"Decide separately who owns parity"*. **The document KNOWS these are unowned and says so in prose, while the column says something that reads as owned — the fact a dispatcher needs is present, in the wrong field. (2) THE CELL NAMES A ROLE, WHICH ASSERTS A HOLDER:** verbatim cells `cluster freeze owner`, `event-loop owner`, `PET input owner`, `PET / cause 5 owner`. **A definite description with no referent — `BEN-380`'s species applied to PEOPLE — and the most misleading of the three unroutable shapes, because a bare area at least looks like an area while a role-name looks like an answer the reader has not yet resolved.** Nobody holds these roles. **THE NEAR-MISS IS THE REASON FOR THE ROW: six rows record who RAISED, FILED, FOUND or CLOSED the item** (`OI-59`, `OI-93`, `OI-94`, `OI-82`, `OI-23`, `OI-65`), **and lifting any of those would have manufactured ownership that does not exist, in a form nothing afterwards could falsify** — a lane name in a `lane/owner` cell with a citation behind it. **`OI-71` says this outright and I had read it:** *"the id is in lane A's block because lane A FILED it, not because lane A owns the subject … Lane A has not answered it and must not."* **A raiser is not an owner and the grammar hides it — *raised by lane C* and *owned by lane C* are one word apart in the same sentence position.** **DONE: 62 → 40, nine cells repaired, EVERY replacement quoting the row it edits and NOTHING assigned to anybody** — one genuine lift, four role-names marked `UNOWNED` with the area preserved, three marked `UNOWNED` quoting their own sentences, `OI-80` routed to `OI-122` (*"OI-122 OWNS THIS ITEM"*), and `OI-128`, **whose cell held a NEXT ACTION rather than an owner** — the displaced sentence is preserved verbatim, not deleted, because it is somebody's content. **`oi_owner_report.py` gained two buckets and 10 tests, and `terminal-no-owner-needed` (12 rows) CORRECTS MY OWN EARLIER NUMBER: a DISCHARGED/SUPERSEDED/WITHDRAWN row needs no owner, so counting it as an ownership defect overstated the backlog.** It reads the state cell's opening, **anchored** — nearly every long row contains *closed* somewhere in its history and an unanchored match would have retired live rows. **93 rows: 28 routable, 12 explicitly unowned, 12 terminal, 40 needing a decision. THE 40 CANNOT BE DISCHARGED BY READING — no owner is recorded anywhere to find, and assigning one is Joseph's or the mediator's call, across 38 DISTINCT area strings, i.e. nearly one decision per row rather than a few bulk assignments.** Three of the 40 are `⚠ ID COLLISION` rows naming *"LANE A's `OI-64`"* — **whose ID it is, not whose item it is**, a third variant of the same conflation, left alone deliberately. **THE RULE: a cell in an owner column must name a party who can be messaged, or say `unowned`; a role, an area, an action, or the name of whoever raised it are all failures, and the last two are worse than an empty cell because they read as answers.** [full](FINDING-20260818-the-word-owner-marks-the-absence-of-one.md) *Seconding lane, on the backlog routed by the mediator.* | `BEN-395`, `BEN-380`, `BEN-080`, `OI-71` | EP-2026-08-18-oi-ownership |

**`draw_1` carries exactly `source`, `trajectory_receipt`, `v` and NO clauses**; and yet `inventory.draws_valid = [1,2,3,4,5]` with `inventory.n = 5`. `VALIDITY.clauses_failing` reads *"none on any of draws **2,3,4,5**"* — **scoped correctly, and the aggregate still reads as five checked draws.** **THREE OF THE EIGHT ARE EXACTLY THE PREMISES `VL130`'s LEDGER TEXT ASSERTS ACROSS ALL FIVE** (*"identical `inputs_sha256`, identical 2,000,000-row `mc_indices`, `bootstrap_seed = -1`"* = clauses `2_target_provenance`, `3_realized_policy`, `5_mc_indices`): **asserted for five, evaluated for four.** **AND `8_execution_environment` IS THE AXIS THE FLOOR MEASURES** — the estimand is residual GPU/process non-determinism at a pinned seed; `member_1` came from job `56847059` and draws 2–5 from `56863958`, so **if the stack or node type differed, draw 1 is a draw from a different process population at identical configuration, and clause 8 is the clause that would have said so.** Never run on it. **THE RULE: an inventory must distinguish CHECKED from ADMITTED.** `draws_present` and `draws_valid` were distinct fields and both read `[1,2,3,4,5]`;
