| BEN-402 | **A DIFFERENCE BETWEEN TWO ARMS THAT THE CONSTRUCTION *REQUIRES* WILL BE READ AS A DISCREPANCY BY ANYONE COMPARING THEM — AND THE NATURAL REMEDY DESTROYS THE MEASUREMENT.** `OI-126` asks whether the Gate-5 nominal's non-containment in its own 50-replica family means `(a)` the estimator is honestly unstable or `(b)` the Poisson bootstrap is not a valid proxy. **`BEN-383` had already found the binary could not express the row's leading candidate — *"the two arms use different Stay-Positive backends"* — and read that candidate as a DISCREPANCY between two arms with other differences ruled out. The loader says it is REQUIRED.** `fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:1462-1487`: *"**Gate 5 requires a negweight-refined target built PER REPLICA (ROOT)**"*, because *"a bootstrap replica draws its own data/background factors, so consuming the nominal array here would silently give every replica the nominal's measured weights and **COLLAPSE THE MEASURED-SIDE VARIANCE**"* — and it is enforced, not intended: `reconcile_gate5_family.py:837-845` requires all 50 `target_sha256_measured` **pairwise distinct** *"because identical targets would read as a SMALL `C_stat` rather than as a failed draw — the reassuring failure"*, `:847-850` that none equals the nominal's. **So harmonising the two arms — the obvious fix for a discrepancy — is the exact operation the reconciler has a check to PREVENT.** **AND THE MECHANISM IS THEN FORCED: the refinement is INSIDE the bootstrap and is NONLINEAR** (Stay-Positive maps possibly-negative measured weights onto the non-negative cone; `:1507` rejects any target carrying negatives), so `nominal = refine(E[w])` while `family mean ≈ E[refine(w)]` and **Jensen makes them differ systematically, one-signed where the curvature is one-signed, largest where the refinement bites hardest — i.e. kinematically localised, with mass moved OUT of those cells and INTO adjacent ones, so the sign FLIPS across the boundary.** Which is the measurement: median `z` = `−0.128` (p‖<6, n=128) → `+3.555` (band, n=84) → `−1.828` (p‖>20, n=45), `nominal/family_mean` = `2.861` in the band, `median(nominal / family MAXIMUM)` = `1.209`, total at the **98th** percentile. **Redistribution, not inflation. Neither branch predicts a sign flip; neither says anything about LOCATION at all.** **THE OTHER HALF, which settles the item even for a reader who rejects the above: `(a)` IS INCOHERENT AS A DISPOSITION INDEPENDENT OF ITS TRUTH.** Its disposition is *"publish the enormous uncertainties as such"* — but the nominal exceeds **every one of the 50 members** in 44 of 63 cells and the family **maximum** by 21% at the median tail cell, so **widening a band cannot capture a point above that band's own maximum.** `(a)` does not dispose of the problem it is offered for. **RULE: before offering a branch set, check that each branch's DISPOSITION would actually resolve the observation — and check whether the difference driving it is REQUIRED by the construction, because a required difference is not a defect and its remedy is a regression.** `BEN-383`'s rule (*a summary's branch set must be able to express the row's leading candidate*) is the shallower form; **this is the case where the candidate itself was mis-typed.** [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-oi126-branch-set-not-exhaustive.md) *Lane C (PET), on the mediator's quorum dispatch; put to quorum rather than to Joseph under his 2026-08-17 two-session grant. `C_stat` is NOT invalidated and `OI-121`/`OI-122` do NOT reopen; what `(c)` leaves live is the narrower `CSTAT-D2` CENTRING question, not the construction.* | `OI-126`, `OI-121` | EP-2026-08-17-oi126-branch-set |
| BEN-403 | **A HYPOTHESIS WHOSE PREDICTIONS ARE READ OFF THE OBSERVATION IT WAS INVENTED TO EXPLAIN CANNOT BE REFUTED BY THAT OBSERVATION — and the symptom is a decision rule that contradicts its own hypothesis.** Lane C proposed `(c)` for `OI-126`: the negweight refinement is inside the bootstrap and nonlinear, so `E[refine(w)] ≠ refine(E[w])` and non-containment is expected. **The code reading was right and is untouched** (`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:1462-1487`, `reconcile_gate5_family.py:837-850`; `BEN-402`). **The FALSIFICATION RULE was broken:** §4 said *"zero total gap **refutes** `(c)`"* while §3 said `(c)` means *"redistribution, not inflation, with the total **nearly conserved**"* — **so a zero total gap was simultaneously the refutation and the prediction.** Caught by lane D, which **declined to return `REFUTED` against a rule whose own hypothesis expected the observed outcome** rather than cashing it. **THE CONTRADICTION IS THE SYMPTOM; THE CAUSE IS THAT THE OBSERVATION WROTE THE PREDICTION.** *"Total nearly conserved"* was never a consequence of Jensen — elementwise-convex Jensen **ADDS** mass and the addition does not cancel under summation, so the mechanism predicts a **positive** total gap. It was a description of the measured nominal total (98th percentile) **imported into the mechanism's predictions**, which made one limb unfalsifiable by the very evidence it existed to explain and left the two limbs mutually inconsistent. **AND A SECOND, INDEPENDENT MISS OF THE SAME FAMILY: I established the mechanism was PRESENT in the construction and never checked it was ACTIVE where the effect is.** D measured it — background share of the measured leg per reco cell is `9.604%` (p‖<6) / **`9.840%` (band)** / `11.368%` (p‖>20), so **the band sits BETWEEN the controls, not at a peak**, and **`0` of 86 band cells** have background mass exceeding the data count. **The cell-level signed sum never goes negative in the band, so Stay-Positive is not clipping there and the nonlinearity is not merely weak but ABSENT.** **AMENDED 2026-08-17, SAME DAY, BY ITS OWN AUTHOR: rule (ii) HAS A CODE-ROUTING INSTANCE AND I COMMITTED IT ONE COMMIT LATER.** Ruling on the data-only ensemble, I prescribed setting `sig_factor = 1` in `train_fullevent_replica.py` because `:202` calls `fe.coherent_bootstrap_factors` — **so the driver MENTIONS the factors and I inferred it APPLIES them. It does not: `:202` re-derives them to VERIFY the loader's (`SystemExit` on mismatch), and `fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:1323,:1325` multiplies `sig_factor` into `w_truth`/`w_reco` ITSELF before returning.** The prescribed override would have changed nothing while the receipts claimed unity — **a false receipt** — and `reconcile_gate5_family.py:526-530` documents that it cannot catch it (*"blind to what the LOADER applied"*). **Found by lane E; the rule violated was mine, filed hours earlier. PRESENCE IS NOT ACTIVITY holds for code exactly as for mechanisms, and E's code-domain form is sharper: *verify the destination PERFORMS the operation rather than merely mentioning it — and PREFER THE LOUD FAILURE.* `OI-61(b)` died to an argparse `choices` list, exit 2, immediate; this one would have passed its own reconciler.** — **TWO RULES. (i) A hypothesis's predictions must be derived from its MECHANISM before the observation is looked at; any prediction that restates the observation is not a prediction, and a rule built on one cannot fail.** (ii) **PRESENCE in the construction is not ACTIVITY in the region — check availability where the effect is, not where the code is.** Related and distinct: `BEN-235`/`BEN-391` are searches too narrow to refute their conclusion; **here the test was well-powered and correctly run, and the HYPOTHESIS was built so that one limb could not lose.** [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-oi126-branch-set-not-exhaustive.md) *Lane C (PET); both defects found by lane D, whose refusal to bank the broken rule is why they were found at all. `(c)` is REFUTED; lane C's type-mismatch and `(a)`-incoherence arguments are untouched and D says so explicitly rather than letting its own hypothesis's death read as support for a rival.* | `OI-126` | EP-2026-08-17-oi126-branch-set |
| BEN-404 | **A GUARD THAT A NEW PRODUCT TRIPS *DELIBERATELY* MUST GET ITS OWN CONTRACT, NEVER A RELAXATION OF THE OLD ONE — and the pressure to relax it arrives disguised as a one-line config change.** A second, **data-only** `C_stat` ensemble (Joseph, *"Do it. I authorize both"*, `9dae576`) is buildable as the existing construction with two of three factor arrays set to exactly `1` — a VALUE change, no pinned file touched, `coherent_bootstrap_factors` (`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:614-625`) already returning three separate arrays that `reconcile_gate5_family.py` hashes separately. **But `reconcile_gate5_family.py:837-845` requires `{label}_sha_all_distinct_across_family` for ALL FOUR labels, *"because identical values here would look like a small statistical component rather than a failed draw"* — so `signal_factor` and `background_factor` shas being identical across all 50 members is EXACTLY the pattern that check exists to catch, except here the collapse is INTENDED.** **RULE: give the new product its own verifier with its own distinctness contract — here `data_factor` and `target` DISTINCT, `signal_factor` and `background_factor` REQUIRED IDENTICAL — because relaxing the shared checker to admit a family that trips it on purpose leaves the OLD family unprotected against the failure the guard was written for.** The reassuring failure and the intended one are byte-identical in the receipt; only the contract distinguishes them. *(`target_sha_all_distinct` still passes — `build_negweight_refined_target:641-655` varies the refined target through `data_factor` alone, so per-replica targets stay distinct at `bkg_factor = 1`. So the checker fails on exactly 2 of its 4 labels, which is the shape that invites a two-line exemption.)* **TWO RIDERS FOUND WHILE RULING.** (i) `PREDECLARATION-20260813:58` — *"`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py` is NOT pinned"*, the sentence that made loader-side work look gate-free — **is STALE**: `BEN-384` found it pinned at `e1402370…` (`run_gate2_target_validator.sh:49` + the Gate-2 runtime receipt), 25 digest sites. **Anyone scoping this product from that predeclaration mis-scopes it.** (ii) `C_MCstat := C_stat^total − C_stat^data` **is not a covariance until PSD-checked** — both families pass through the SAME trained network, so the stream perturbations are not independent and `C_total ≠ C_data + C_MC` in general. **Named before it happens because I committed the same error family the same afternoon** (`BEN-403`): the arithmetic of subtraction is not the physics of decomposition. [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-cstat-is-a-second-product.md) *Lane C (PET), step 1 of the data-only dispatch; also corrects the dispatch's premise that a data-only family has no nominal/replica training asymmetry — the DATA factors are `Poisson(1)` too, so `1/e` = 36.8% of data rows carry weight zero and the thinned-support asymmetry survives.* | `OI-126`, `OI-94` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-410 | **A COMMAND YOU HAVE NOT EXECUTED IS STILL A DESCRIPTION — and this one was authorized by the principal, specified by the owning lane, reviewed by the mediator, and does not exist.** The `CODE_ROOT` sync was scoped, put to Joseph as a yes/no naming the exact string, approved, and dispatched as `git pull --ff-only origin main`. On the cluster: **`fatal: 'origin' does not appear to be a git repository`.** `git remote` there returns **`analysis-note` and `github`** — verified independently by me after the mediator hit it. **Three parties reviewed a command that could not run, because all three of us reasoned about it and none of us ran it.** **THE UNCOMFORTABLE PART, AND IT IS THE REASON THIS IS A FINDING RATHER THAN A TYPO: this is `CLAUDE.md`'s own principle failing INSIDE its area of strength.** *"Prefer the executable form of any rule you are tempted to write down"* is what made us write a command instead of *"bring the cluster tree up to date"* — and the executable form's whole claim is that it cannot be vague. **It can still be WRONG, and it is wrong in a way prose is not: prose degrades to a human re-deriving the step, while a wrong command is followed exactly and fails, or worse succeeds differently.** A remote name is environment state, and every command carries some; ours carried one and we all defaulted it from the tree we were each sitting in. **Rule: an authorization that names a command must be obtained on a command someone has RUN — dry-run it, or authorize the OUTCOME and let the executor pick the invocation. A specific command in an authorization looks like precision and is a second thing to get wrong.** **Found only because the executor re-derived the step-1 conditions instead of trusting a several-hour-old measurement**, which is the discipline that also caught `BEN-389`'s three instances; the defect fell out of the re-derivation rather than being looked for. **Not a sync defect: the sync succeeded once the remote was named correctly, `5fb7e38` → `7ac36ac`, four dirty tracked files untouched, 0 modified `.py`/`.sh`.** [full](../OPEN_ITEMS.md) `OI-74` *Lane E; hit by the mediator session.* | `BEN-389`, `BEN-382`, `BEN-112` | EP-2026-08-17-cluster-sync |
| BEN-411 | **A CONSTRAINT ISSUED TO KEEP AN EDIT OFF A PINNED FILE ROUTED IT TO A FILE THAT DOES NOT PERFORM THE OPERATION — and the target file's own comment says so.** `C_stat^data` was ruled to be built with the override *"in the replica driver, NEVER inside `coherent_bootstrap_factors`"* — a sound instinct, aimed at keeping the diff off `fullevent_fps_dataloader.py`, which is pinned 25 ways. **It cannot work for the signal stream.** `fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:1321-1325` draws the factors and **multiplies `sig_factor` into `w_truth`/`w_reco` itself** before returning; `train_fullevent_replica.py:202-211` merely **re-derives them to VERIFY the loader's**, asserting equality and raising on mismatch. **A checker, not an applier — so `sig_factor = 1` in the driver changes nothing.** **AND IT IS NOT UNDOABLE AFTERWARDS: 36.8% of Poisson(1) factors are exactly zero (1/e), so the multiply DESTROYS information rather than scaling it** — no post-hoc division recovers the unthinned weights. **Route A would therefore produce a family whose RECEIPTS claim unity while the TRAINING consumed thinned MC**, and `reconcile_gate5_family.py:526-530` states in its own note that it *"compares the BUILDER's recomputation to this tool's redraw, so it is **blind to what the LOADER applied**"* — **the instrument that would catch the lie documents that it cannot.** **Third site today for `BEN-386`** (`OI-61(b)`'s argparse `choices`, `OI-96`'s coverage grep, this) **and the first where the wrong-file route would have SUCCEEDED SILENTLY rather than failing loudly** — argparse exits 2; this one ships a false receipt. **Rule: when routing an edit away from a pinned file, verify the destination PERFORMS the operation, not merely that it MENTIONS it — and prefer the failure mode that is loud. A route that cannot work and cannot be detected is worse than a blocked one.** **VIABLE ROUTE FOUND AND IT IS OFF THE PINNED FILES: call the loader with `bootstrap_seed=None`** (`:1332-1334` returns unthinned MC), the driver already intercepting the loader call, with the measured stream applied where it is already driver-level. Costed to BUILD in the determination — four sites, one of them a new verdict path in a pin-exposed reconciler — **and NOT called cheap, per this lane's own rule that a sweep can show an item is expensive and never that it is cheap.** **Also recorded: the reconciler needs TWO fixes, not the one flagged.** Its `:519-530` replay re-draws all three streams and compares hashes, so unity mismatches **before** the `:837-845` distinctness labels are ever reached — profile `2 of 4 distinctness plus 2 of 3 replay`, which invites a short exemption even more strongly than the ruling anticipated. [full](DETERMINATION-20260817-cstat-data-only-route-and-cost-to-build.md), probe `state/probe-cstat-data-only-route-20260817.py` *Lane E; constraint from lane C's ruling.* | `BEN-386`, `BEN-255`, `BEN-384`, `BEN-112` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-405 | **A GUARD WHOSE ABSENT-DEFAULT COLLIDES WITH A MEANINGFUL DOMAIN VALUE STOPS GUARDING EXACTLY WHEN THAT VALUE IS IN USE — and the failure it lets through resurfaces wearing someone else's name.** `train_fullevent_replica.py:197`: `if int(bootstrap.get("bootstrap_seed", -1)) != int(args.bootstrap_seed): raise SystemExit("loader bootstrap evidence carries the wrong seed")`. **The absent-default is `-1`, and `-1` is this pipeline's own sentinel for *no bootstrap*** — `VL130`'s verified floor premises are *"identical inputs, identical 2,000,000-row `mc_indices`, **`bootstrap_seed = -1`**"*. **So a run invoked with `--bootstrap-seed -1` against an EMPTY bootstrap block compares `-1 != -1`, which is False, and the guard PASSES VACUOUSLY** (verified). It dies five lines on at `:202` with `ValueError: expected non-negative integer` from `np.random.default_rng(-1)` (verified) — **loud but MISATTRIBUTED: the message names an RNG problem, so a reader debugging it looks at numpy and not at the missing loader evidence.** **AND THE EMPTINESS IS MANUFACTURED BY A DEFENSIVE IDIOM.** `:196` is `dict(meta.get("bootstrap") or {})`, same at `:220` and `:253`. **A `.get` default fires only when the KEY IS ABSENT, never when its value is `None`** — so `"bootstrap": null` would raise `AttributeError` at the reader (`reconcile_gate5_family.py:355`, `bs = r.get("bootstrap", {})`) and be **LOUD**, while `or {}` converts `ABSENT` into `EMPTY` at the writer and makes the same condition **SILENT**. **`{}` is empty in content and present in type — the one shape that defeats both a presence check and an exception.** Mechanism established by the mediator; both halves verified here. **TWO RULES. (i) A guard's absent-default must be a value no legal input can take** — `None` with an explicit `is None` test, never a number the domain already uses. **(ii) A defensive `or {}` at a WRITER is not defensive: it destroys the distinction its reader's own `.get` default exists to catch.** Robustness idioms belong where a value is consumed, not where it is recorded. **Same class as `BEN-395` (a populated `lane/owner` cell reading as ownership) and `BEN-247` (a partially-`COMPLETED` job id satisfying a completeness check) — a non-empty value satisfying a check an empty one would have failed, here in its purest form because the non-empty value IS `{}`.** **LATENT, NOT LIVE — AND HERE THAT INVERTS THE PRIORITY RATHER THAN LOWERING IT.** Qualification supplied by the mediator and verified here: **no current invocation path passes `-1` to the REPLICA driver.** The only real invoker is `sbatch_gate5_replica_train_array.sh:30,:70` — `SEED=$((50000 + INDEX))`, `--bootstrap-seed "$SEED"`, so seeds are `50000`–`50049` and never the sentinel. And the launcher that DOES run at `-1` never reaches this guard: `sbatch_pet_fullevent_floor_replicate_array.sh:56` sets `DRIVER` to **`train_fullevent_nominal.py`**, its only mention of the replica driver is a comment at `:132` (*"Shape copied from train_fullevent_replica.py:347-353"*), and it passes no `--bootstrap-seed` at all. **SO THIS IS `Route B`'s TRAP SPECIFICALLY: the collision is real, and what is missing today is an invocation that passes a no-bootstrap value to the replica driver — WHICH IS EXACTLY WHAT THE DATA-ONLY ROUTE INTRODUCES.** It is not a defect `Route B` must avoid tripping over; **it is a defect `Route B` would ACTIVATE, dormant only because nobody has yet built the thing that reaches it.** **THIRD RULE, and it is the reusable one: *"no current caller"* normally lowers a defect's priority, and it INVERTS when the work that would create the caller is already authorized.** Dormancy is a fact about today's call graph, not about risk; **read it against the next authorized step before triaging it down.** **CONSEQUENCE, recorded in the ruling: `Route B` through the driver AS IT STANDS fails LOUDLY at `:197` for any real replica seed, so the silence is in the reconciler and not in the pipeline as built. The silent path is the DRIVER EDIT `Route B` requires — and the smallest-looking way to make `:197` accept a bootstrap-free run is to relax it, which is exactly the edit that arms the reader. So the edit must BRANCH ON THE PRODUCT, never relax the check.** [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-cstat-is-a-second-product.md) *Lane C (PET), found while sharpening the data-only ruling's `Route B` requirement; `BEN-404`'s rule with a line number.* | `OI-126`, `OI-94` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-406 | **TWO PINS ON ONE FILE ARE A DEFECT ONLY IF BOTH CLAIM THE SAME TENSE — and comparing a historical RECORD against a forward-looking GUARD manufactures a false positive that looks exactly like drift.** Raised as a live discrepancy: `sbatch_pet_fullevent_floor_replicate_array.sh:101,:117` pins `train_fullevent_nominal.py` at `91144bee…` while an untracked `STEP1_DYNAMICS` runtime receipt carries `66aa1f8f…` for the same file. **NOT A DISAGREEMENT.** The launcher's is a **PRECONDITION ASSERTION** — its own header says *"a floor is only a floor under the same code"* — **present tense about a future run.** The receipt's is a **RUNTIME RECORD**: `driver` / `driver_sha256` / `driver_output` / `driver_output_sha256`, i.e. *"this code ran and produced this"*, **past tense.** Two claims about two moments. Raised and closed by the mediator; the tense rule is lane C's and the closure vindicates it. **METHOD RIDER, `BEN-235`'s shape caught PROSPECTIVELY for once:** lane C's covering grep over the tracked tree found all **7** `name`-then-`sha` pins of that file agreeing on `91144bee…`, and **predicted its own blind spot before the answer arrived** — the pattern matches only the *adjacency* form and would miss `path` and `digest` in **separate JSON fields**, which is exactly the receipt's shape (and the receipt is untracked besides, one of the 721). **So the null was reported as evidence about the search and ROUTED rather than recorded**, and the prediction was confirmed. *A null whose blind spot you can name is a different object from a null you present as an answer.* **AND THE GATE ALREADY KNOWS THIS CLASS, WHICH CORRECTS THE FRAMING THE OBSERVATION ARRIVED WITH.** `verify_hash_bindings.py:370` compares `sha256(current file)` against the pinned value, so a runtime record must mismatch once its file legitimately changes — but `:78-80`'s `KNOWN_PREEXISTING` comment states the rationale verbatim: *"these receipts record what ran at submit time, and **rewriting their hashes would falsify history**."* **The gate is not semantically confused; its exemption list is scoped to drift *before 2026-07-28* while the repo has no end date.** So each legitimate post-cutoff change to a pinned file produces a mismatch of the SAME SPECIES as those four entries with nowhere to be recorded. **The remedy is additive provenance, not a repin — bind the historical bytes to the IMMUTABLE COMMIT that carried them rather than to the live path**, which is the pattern lane C already used when it declined to extend `KNOWN_PREEXISTING` for exactly this cutoff reason. **CONSEQUENCE FOR TRIAGE: a binding mismatch is not automatically a thing to FIX — one may be a receipt doing its job.** **RULE, and it constrains new verifiers rather than only old ones: a check must compare like tense to like.** Assert either **present** (tree vs tree) or **past self-contained** (a receipt asserting agreement between two things IT recorded at one moment). **Never present-vs-historical without an exemption channel that has no expiry.** Applied immediately: `C_stat^data`'s verdict path may contain no cross-tense comparison. [full](FINDING-20260817-two-pins-one-file-two-tenses.md) *Lane C (PET), tense rule and method; instance raised and closed by the mediator, which resolved it one `ssh` away rather than leaving it routed. Gate framing and the `KNOWN_PREEXISTING` scope read here; NOT filed against the gate, which belongs to its owner with D's `1409/2` baseline.* | `OI-126` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-412 | **THE MEDIAN AND THE LOW TAIL GAVE OPPOSITE ANSWERS TO THE SAME QUESTION, AND THE MEDIAN WAS THE WRONG ONE — caught only because the dispatch forbade reducing before reporting.** `OI-126`'s surviving mechanism needed the 6–20 GeV band to be the MC-sparse end of the grid: Poisson(1) support thinning is uniform IN SHARE (**measured: surviving distinct fraction 0.63205 band, 0.63253 outside, against 1−1/e = 0.63212**), so a band-confined effect requires a LOCAL AMPLIFIER, and the only one available is sparsity. **Measured on the EXACT training population** (`mc_indices`, 2 000 000 of 49 152 885 rows) with the band from the artifact's own `reported_bin_mask`: **band median 2 382 rows/cell against 5 506 outside — 2.3× SPARSER BY MEDIAN, which reads as "mechanism plausible" and would have sent someone to build a four-site route.** **AND IT IS WRONG, because the amplifier acts through the LOW TAIL: band min 42 against outside 1, band q25 912 against 421, and cells below 100 rows are 4/84 = 4.8% in the band against 27/174 = 15.5% outside — starvation is 3.2× MORE prevalent OUTSIDE the band at the tightest threshold, and more prevalent outside at every threshold tried.** A cell with 2 382 rows losing 36.8% is not in trouble; a cell with 1 row is. **So the amplifier sits OUTSIDE the band while the effect is confined TO it — the opposite of the prediction, and the mechanism dies by availability exactly as the Jensen route did on 0 of 86 cells.** **THE TRANSFERABLE PART IS NOT "medians hide tails", which everyone already agrees with: it is that THE STATISTIC MUST BE CHOSEN FROM THE MECHANISM'S OWN ACTING CHANNEL. This mechanism acts by severity in thin cells, so its operand is the low tail, and ANY central statistic — median, mean, quartile spread — is measuring a quantity the mechanism does not use.** Asking "sparse compared to what, through what channel" is the question, and "is the band sparser" was underspecified in a way that had a confident wrong answer available. **Fifth instance today of a summary statistic standing in for a distribution and the FIRST caught before it propagated;** the per-cell arrays are published so nobody must trust this row's cut points. **ALSO: the answer FLIPS with the population.** A first pass on the full 49.2M-row inventory inverted the ordering (band 57 410 vs 127 073); the full inventory is not what the estimator trains on. **A conclusion that depends on which population you pick must be computed on the one the estimator consumes, and both edge arrays were asserted equal to the production grid before indexing, or the cell index would not address the artifact's own mask.** **NOT KILLED: the smoothness claim itself, and the comparability argument — the data factors are Poisson(1) too, so the nominal still trains on the full inventory while every replica trains thinned. The ensemble stays warranted; its predeclaration must stop citing MC-thinning as the motivation.** [full](DETERMINATION-20260817-oi126-band-is-not-the-mc-sparse-end.md), receipt `state/oi126-band-mc-occupancy-20260817.json` *Lane E; premise and ordering from lane C and the mediator.* | `BEN-411`, `BEN-386` | EP-2026-08-17-oi126-availability |
| BEN-407 | **AN UNSATISFIABLE GUARD MAY BE CHECKING THE WRONG PROPOSITION, NOT A TRUE ONE THE NEW PRODUCT FAILS — AND THE DIFFERENCE DECIDES BETWEEN SKIPPING IT AND REPLACING IT WITH SOMETHING STRONGER.** `fe.validate_coherent_bootstrap` (`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:750`), called fail-closed from `train_fullevent_replica.py:291` AND `extract_fullevent_replica.py:138`, **cannot be satisfied by a data-only family, structurally**: `:759` `need = {"mc_indices", "sig_bootstrap_factor", "bootstrap_seed"}` makes the signal factor **MANDATORY** while `:772` makes background **CONDITIONAL** — so background is escapable by omission and signal is not — and `:768` raises unless the persisted factor equals `mc_poisson_factor(n_sig_full, seed)[imc]`. **All three dispositions fail: omit → missing-key raise; persist unity → not-canonical raise; persist the real draw → the receipt claims thinned MC over unthinned training, which is `Route A`'s defect relocated.** **THE RULING TURNS ON WHAT `:768` ASSERTS, NOT ON WHETHER IT PASSES.** Its content is *"the persisted factor IS the canonical draw at these indices"* — a **coherence** claim, proving no post-subset redraw. **It says NOTHING about whether the draw was APPLIED** (that is the loader's `:1323` multiply, which `reconcile_gate5_family.py:526-530` states no guard covers). **So for a data-only family the guard is checking the WRONG PROPOSITION — there is no signal draw to be coherent with — and the property the product needs is that NO draw was applied, which is STRONGER.** **RULE: before skipping an unsatisfiable guard, read its predicate. If the new product fails a TRUE claim, skipping is a relaxation and forbidden; if the claim is INAPPLICABLE, the replacement can and must be stronger than what it displaces.** Here: coherence **re-pointed** at the one stream that varies (data), plus `hash_array(w_truth) == hash_array(w_truth_full[imc])`, which no existing guard has. **Net, the data-only path is verified more tightly than the three-stream path.** **THREE COROLLARIES FILED WITH IT.** (i) **The predicates DIFFER per call site and the difference is forced**, not stylistic: `w_truth` is not among the artifact's 45 keys, so train asserts it **live** (present tense) and extract asserts the two persisted hashes **against each other** (past self-contained) — `BEN-406` forbids extract re-deriving from the current input, which would decay to `FAIL` on a legitimate input re-dump. (ii) **`extract` needs the branch at TWO places**: `:150-155` independently raises on full-length factors vs canonical replay, so a ruling covering only `:138` would have left it failing anyway. (iii) **The write-time raise IS the gate — no pre-run loader call is required**: `P5` is asserted inside `replica_atomic` before the write, so a thinned-MC replica **never comes into existence**. | BEN-408 | **A NORMALIZE-TO-CONSTANT STEP DIVIDES OUT THE FLUCTUATION ITS INPUT CARRIED, SO ONE SCALAR BECOMES THE *ONLY* ROUTE BY WHICH A RESAMPLED COUNT REACHES THE ANSWER — AND FREEZING IT DELETES THE LEADING STATISTICAL TERM BY EXACT CANCELLATION RATHER THAN ATTENUATING IT.** The data-only `C_stat` route calls the loader with `bootstrap_seed=None`, which **nulls all three factors, not just the MC ones** (`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:1332-1334`), and at `:945-951` `data_factor is None → n_data_eff = float(n_data_rows)` — **the raw row count, no error** — so the class ratio `R` reverts to nominal (`:1545-1549`, `:1552`). **The precomputed refined target DOES carry the data draw, and `normalize=True` then cancels it exactly:** `omnifold_nn/omnifold/dataloader.py:149-153` computes `_c = normalization_factor/sumw` and multiplies, so the measured block's total weight is **exactly `1e6·R`** whatever the weights summed to. **Freeze `R` and all fifty replicas share one measured normalization.** **THE CONSEQUENCE IS NOT A BIASED-LOW `σ_stat`, IT IS A DIFFERENT QUANTITY: a SHAPE-ONLY statistical uncertainty published under a name the field reads as total-rate.** Which is exactly the defect `BEN-400` disqualified a ratio for the same afternoon (`VL130` shape-only because `central_vector` sums to 1) — **except this one is quoted to T2K/MicroBooNE/NOvA, and it defeats the external-comparability justification the 151 A100-h was authorized on.** So the cheap option was refused **on the merits**, not deprioritised: *documenting a bias is not correcting it, and a reader comparing against another experiment does not read our predeclaration.* **THE REUSABLE RULE, from resolving the bit-exactness trap the fix introduces: ASSERT BIT-EXACTNESS WHERE THE CLAIM IS THAT NOTHING HAPPENED, AND A TOLERANCED CLOSURE WHERE THE CLAIM IS THAT A SPECIFIC COMPUTATION HAPPENED.** An absence has no rounding, so bit-exactness is its complete predicate; an arithmetic result does, so bit-exactness there **checks the implementation PATH rather than the claim** and fails for reasons with no physics content. Applied: MC leg bit-exact to `w_truth_full[imc]`; measured leg `sum(weight[pass_reco])` vs `1e6·R_dataonly` to `≤ 4·float32 eps`; identity to an unreachable one-shot construction **explicitly waived**. The two requirements touch **disjoint arrays**, so a lane finding both must not pick one. *(Size, so it is not re-litigated from the word "non-bit-exact": `≲ 2·eps = 2.4e-7`, common-mode within a replica, against a `5.167%` family spread — `2.3e-6` of the measured quantity.)* **THIRD CONSEQUENCE OF THE SAME OVERLOAD, LIVE 2026-08-17 19:52Z: the authorized data-only GPU family `57194055` failed closed at `assert_refined_target_is_replica` (`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:742` via `train_fullevent_replica.py:288`) — `ValueError: refined target has bootstrap_seed=None (NOMINAL)`. The guard reads `bootstrap_seed` to answer an IDENTITY question (*is this target this replica's or the nominal's*) while the data-only design sets it as a CONSTRUCTION SWITCH (*leave the MC unthinned*). A TRUE claim producing a FALSE POSITIVE, so the rule is re-target not relax — and the loader's own cross-seed block at `:1479-1493` is unreachable under `bootstrap_seed=None`, making the driver assert the SOLE binding. Ruled: the fix is necessary, INSUFFICIENT without `T4` (which gives the data-only seed its own single-meaning key and stops the guard consulting the overloaded one), and correctly located at the driver because the shared assert is in the PINNED loader.** **AND THE NAMING VARIANT, third instance today of one class:** `bootstrap_seed=None` **reads** as *no bootstrap* and **means** *all three streams off and the measured normalization silently reverts to nominal*. **A keyword's NAME describes intent; only its body describes effect** — found by lane E tracing what the keyword does rather than trusting what it is called, which is `BEN-403(ii)`'s *presence is not activity* in the naming domain. *(Also: `n_data_effective` was `OI-90`'s evidence that the data factor WAS applied, and `:945-951` is the mechanism by which it silently would NOT be — the same field load-bearing in both directions, `BEN-405`'s class in the quantity a `σ_stat` depends on.)* [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-normalization-must-vary.md) *Lane C (PET). ASSERT ON IT AT A LATER POINT OF USE.** Lane C specified `P5` as *"`hash_array(w_truth) == hash_array(w_truth_full[imc])`"* for the data-only family. **It can never pass, even with the MC completely unthinned:** `fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:1348-1352` builds the MC `DataLoader` with `normalize=True, normalization_factor=1e6`, and `omnifold_nn/omnifold/dataloader.py:148-156` rescales in place by `_c = normalization_factor/sumw` — **with `_src = weight_reco`, not `weight`** — so `mc.weight == w_truth_full[imc] · 1e6 / sum(w_reco[pass_reco])`, and nothing records `_c`, so the pre-normalization vector is not recoverable either. **THE LOADER'S OWN COMMENT THREE LINES ABOVE THE CALL SAYS IT:** *"The truth leg lands at `1e6*sum(w_truth)/sum(w_reco)` by construction; truth-space YIELDS must be built from the raw weights times pot_scale, **never from `mc.weight`**"* (`:1344-1347`). **So the predicate asserted an identity the code's own adjacent comment forbids** — `BEN-239`, evidence sitting unread, in a comment I would have hit by scrolling. **THE ERROR NAMED PRECISELY, and the sequence matters: `P5` (`0e99adb`) PREDATES `BEN-408`'s bit-exact-vs-toleranced rule (`9e63258`), so this is NOT the stater violating their own rule — it is the rule, once stated, retroactively diagnosing an earlier predicate, which is the rule WORKING.** Recording it the other way round would be a more flattering story and a false one. **What IS mine: I specified a predicate over `w_truth` by its identity at the point it is CREATED, not at the point the predicate COMPARES it, with a normalization running in between** — `BEN-408`'s naming variant one level in, applied to an array rather than a keyword. **THE REPAIR IS A STRENGTHENING, NOT A CONCESSION.** `P5a`: `np.array_equal(mc.weight == 0, w_truth_full[imc] == 0)` — **BIT-EXACT, and the discriminating half**, because `Poisson(1)` zeroes **36.73%** of rows (`1/e = 36.79%`) while **a positive scalar zeroes exactly `0.00000%`** (measured at `c = 0.5`, `2.0`, `1e-3`). **The zero pattern IS the signature of thinning and is immune to every scalar transformation downstream.** `P5b`: proportionality to ONE positive scalar with the scalar **DERIVED INDEPENDENTLY** as `1e6/sum(w_reco_full[imc][pass_reco])` rather than read back, to `≤ 4·eps·c` over nonzero rows. **Together they catch THREE failure modes where `P5` aimed at one — thinning, per-row factor, and WRONG NORMALIZATION — the third bought entirely by deriving the scalar rather than reading it.** **THREE ADDITIONS THE SPLIT NEEDED, all lane C's.** (i) **`P5b` asserts `size == 1` ITSELF**: `sumw` is over the rank-slice, so the derived scalar is a different quantity under multi-rank, and the launchers' single-rank guard is external — a predicate depending on one is `BEN-386`'s shape. (ii) **A NAMED negative control that `c` derived from the TRUTH leg must FAIL** — a control against the *plausible* mistake, since `_src = weight_reco` and a truth-leg control would pass on a wrong constant. (iii) **`P5a` MUST FAIL on a flush-to-zero and that is CORRECT** — `float32(1e-45)·0.5 = 0.0` is reachable, so a subnormal weight can be destroyed and appear as a new zero; **a future lane must NOT "repair" `P5a` by excluding zero rows of `mc.weight`, because that exempts exactly the failure mode.** Foreclosed here before it is attempted. | BEN-413 | **THE INSTANCE OF `BEN-409`, WHICH LANE C FILED FIRST AND WHICH STATES THE CLASS — recorded separately only for the mechanism and the sequence, NOT as an independent discovery. A MANDATORY PREDICATE THAT CANNOT PASS IS A HARD FAILURE WEARING A CHECK'S CLOTHES — and the loader's own comment ruled out the identity it asserted, three lines above the call.** `C_stat^data`'s spec made P5 `hash_array(w_truth) == hash_array(w_truth_full[imc])` mandatory at the training site. **It is false even with the MC completely unthinned:** the MC DataLoader is built `normalize=True, normalization_factor=1e6`, so the truth leg lands at `1e6*sum(w_truth)/sum(w_reco)` BY CONSTRUCTION, and `fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:1344-1347` says so in prose — *"truth-space yields must be built from the raw weights times pot_scale, NEVER FROM mc.weight"* — immediately above the construction. **The predicate had ZERO discriminating power: not weak, not conservative, simply unable to pass**, so shipping it would have converted a guard into a guaranteed red and then into a waiver. **THE REPAIR IS THE STATER'S OWN RULE APPLIED TO ITS OWN EARLIER PREDICATE: assert BIT-EXACTNESS where the claim is that NOTHING HAPPENED, and a TOLERANCED CLOSURE where the claim is that A SPECIFIC COMPUTATION HAPPENED.** P5 bundled both — no Poisson thinning (an absence, no rounding) and one scalar renormalization (an arithmetic result, which has rounding). Split: **P5a** compares the ZERO PATTERN bit-exactly, because Poisson(1) zeroes ~1/e of rows and a positive scalar zeroes none; **P5b** asserts proportionality to <= 4 float32 eps with the scalar DERIVED INDEPENDENTLY, so it also catches a WRONG normalization that a hash comparison could not. **Three failure modes against the one P5 aimed at.** **THE SEQUENCE MATTERS AND IS RECORDED AGAINST THE DAY'S PATTERN: P5 landed 11 minutes BEFORE the bit-exact-vs-toleranced rule, so this is not a lane violating a rule it had just filed — it is THE RULE, ONCE STATED, RETROACTIVELY DIAGNOSING AN EARLIER PREDICATE.** Inflating the day's three genuine self-violations with a fourth that is actually the rule working would devalue the real ones. **What the author's error narrowly WAS: specifying a predicate over `w_truth` by its identity AT THE POINT IT IS CREATED rather than at the point the predicate COMPARES it, with a normalization running in between.** Rule: **a predicate over a mutable array must name the array's state AT THE COMPARISON, not at its birth** — and before making one mandatory, run it once against a correct input. It cannot, and that is the wrong property to want: `57194055_[22-31]` were `PENDING (DependencyNeverSatisfied)`, which Slurm neither runs nor (with `kill_invalid_depend` unset) removes, **so the condition was unsatisfiable and I reported *"still draining"***. **A GUARD'S CONDITION IS A PREDICATE ON THE PRESENT; A WATCH NEEDS ONE ON REACHABILITY** -- *no* is always a correct answer to *may I submit now?*, and the two differ exactly when the blocking state is TERMINAL BUT NOT TERMINATING. Repair: fire on EITHER terminal shape and SAY WHICH -- `EMPTY` (guard will pass) or `BLOCKED` (never will; a DECISION, not a wait) -- which converts an unbounded wait into a named question, and matters because the remedy was a `scancel` inside a standing prohibition. Re-armed, it fired in one poll, which is the measurement proving the old one would have polled indefinitely. Also measured, and NOT diagnosed: the block was not the `aftercorr` consequence of the one failed target -- `_42`'s training task released and RAN -- but a contiguous ten, one `%10` release window; suggestive is as far as the evidence goes. Cost zero: those ten produced nothing and the family is discarded. `BEN-028`'s mirror -- there silence meant healthy, here stuck; both say pick an observable that DISTINGUISHES the states, not one that correlates with the expected one. (1) A **RUNNING** task's `Elapsed` read as its duration: 20 min quoted, 43 min actual from COMPLETED siblings, **2.8x low**, and a wait-vs-cancel decision was already made on it. (2) `50 x 43 min` summed and labelled **node-hours** on a `shared_*` partition: `AllocTRES billing=36,cpu=36,node=1` of a 128-core node makes the charged figure `35.70 x 36/128 = 10.04`, so **3.6x high** -- and `node=1` means one node was ALLOCATED, not billed, which is the phrase that makes the wrong reading feel supported. (3) `Elapsed` used to predict DRAIN when dispatch is queue-limited: `Start` deltas show tasks arriving in **PAIRS every ~6.7 min despite `%10`**, giving 2.3-7 h against my *"couple of minutes"* -- **50-140x low**, and my refusal to spend a delegated authorization rested entirely on it. **In all three the right field was ONE AWAY in the same `sacct` output**, needing only a different filter, a second field, or a different column. **THE TELL IS THAT THE NUMBER CAME OUT CONVENIENT** -- three for three it flattered the answer I was already inclined toward -- and none of them looks like a failure: a running job's `Elapsed` is not an error or a null, it is a smaller TRUE number, and re-running the same command never corrects it. Checks: name the question then pick the field; `--state=COMPLETED` for durations; `AllocTRES` for cost; `Start` deltas for drains; and re-derive by a second route whenever the number is convenient. [full](FINDING-20260818-the-scheduler-reports-five-times-and-i-kept-picking-the-easiest.md) *Lane E.* | `BEN-027`, `BEN-471`, `BEN-472` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-474 | **AN INSTRUMENT'S CONDITIONS ARE PART OF THE MEASUREMENT: STRICTER IS A FALSE ALARM, LOOSER IS A FALSE PASS, AND NEITHER APPEARS IN ITS OUTPUT.** Two verification scripts, hours apart, opposite directions. **LOOSER:** the run-bound addendum's zero-artifact refusal -- the check that turns *"before the artifact exists"* from a promise into a condition -- ran from the local checkout against a `/pscratch` family root absent on that filesystem, so the walk found zero artifacts and **the refusal could not fire.** The cluster state was clean, so the claim was TRUE **by timing, not because the check established it**, and telling those apart is the check's entire purpose. **STRICTER:** an activation smoke failed three times on a healthy system -- `set -u` the launcher does not set (line 15), then `env -i` when the controller submits `--export=ALL`, then a conda `ADDR2LINE: unbound variable` that **2 of generation one's 50 tasks emitted and COMPLETED**, a fact available only from the HISTORICAL POPULATION and not from my own shell. Repairs: refuse when the root is not a directory FROM HERE, plus `--repo` so the script can run where the root is visible instead of being forced to live beside the code it reads; and where faithfulness was unreachable, **NARROW THE CLAIM RATHER THAN WIDEN THE CHECK** -- the encodable property is the gate, activation is INFO, because **a gate that fails on a healthy system is worse than none**: it gets switched off exactly as fast as an under-reporting one gets trusted (lane B). The looser direction is worse because it licenses action; the stricter one is worse because it gets the check deleted, which licenses the same action later with nothing watching. Test to steal: **could this have failed, here, today?** **AMENDMENT, same day, and it is a SEPARATE CLAUSE rather than another instance: AN OVER-STRICT INSTRUMENT RAISES A FALSE ALARM; AN OVER-STRICT *SHIM* KILLS THE JOB.** Forty minutes after filing this row I wrote `set -u` into a production shim in the data root -- for hygiene -- and it killed array `57235710` 50/50 in ten seconds: the activator it exists to source reaches conda's `activate-binutils_linux-64.sh`, which references `ADDR2LINE` unbound, and under `-u` that is fatal to the whole shell while `source` runs in the CALLER's shell. Same error as the two instrument instances above, **one category worse, because a shim is ON the execution path rather than beside it** -- so the identical mistake goes from a false alarm to a dead array. Isolated by execution, not reading: sourcing the shim died and sourcing the real activator did not, and the shim's only difference was that line. **AND THE COROLLARY THAT COST MORE THAN THE OUTAGE:** I had told the orchestrator activation *"could not be made a reliable gate"*, it accepted that and published it to the principal as a named scope limit -- and it was **true of my test, not of the subject**. All three of my activation failures (`set -u`, `env -i`, reading a conda warning as fatal) were conditions the job does not have. **A published limitation can be an artifact of the instrument, and the check that would have caught it was one `grep` on the launcher's own shell options.** [full](FINDING-20260818-an-instrument-must-reproduce-its-subjects-conditions.md) *Lane E.* | `BEN-470`, `BEN-418`, `BEN-258` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-420 | **A DEFAULT'S SAFETY IS A PROPERTY OF THE CALL GRAPH, NOT OF THE VALUE — so a *safe* default becomes an unmarked assumption the instant a second branch is authorized, and the enumeration that would catch it is available at AUTHORIZATION time and gets done at SUBMISSION time.** Joseph ruled *"do it"*; the go was relayed; **lane E stopped before `sbatch`.** `--cstat-product` exists only on `train_fullevent_replica.py:531` with `default=CSTAT_THREE_STREAM`, and **no launcher passes it** — `grep -c cstat-product` returns **0** for `submit_gate5_replica_n50.sh`, `sbatch_gate5_replica_train_array.sh` and `sbatch_gate5_replica_target_array.sh`. **So the submission would have spent 151 A100-hours rebuilding the product that already exists, silently: the artifacts pass, the reconciler verdicts them, and the only thing distinguishing them is a `cstat_product: three-stream-v1` field nobody would think to read.** **`absence → three-stream` was the direction all three of us agreed was safe, and it WAS — for every existing launcher. It is the same value that makes the authorized branch unreachable.** *(Unifies with `BEN-405`'s third rule: dormancy is a fact about today's call graph, not about risk.)* **COROLLARY, the actionable half: when a second branch is authorized, enumerate every default that DISCRIMINATES between the branches — at authorization time. Here it was one `grep` over three files, available the moment the go was given.** **RULED, and the loudness is bought WITHOUT a pinned edit:** a *required* flag would force edits to **both PINNED array launchers** to pass `three-stream-v1` — purchasing the exact pinned edit this whole route exists to avoid. **Instead: `L1` the data-only launchers write to a DISJOINT family root** (all three existing launchers hardcode `fullevent_cstat_n50` at `:13,:14,:32` / `:13,:14,:34` / `:15`), **and `L2` the drivers assert TAG ⟺ ROOT and raise on mismatch.** That converts *"a field nobody would read"* into a check that reads it, and the existing 50 artifacts already occupy their root under `overwrite=False`, so a wrong-launcher submission **collides loudly** instead of rebuilding. **NEW launchers, never edits** — three-stream launchers stay byte-identical, and `CLAUDE.md`'s rename/delete prohibition is satisfied because this **ADDS**. **Forward prohibition recorded: the pair must NEVER be unified into one parametrised launcher, because that edit arrives as a refactor.** **AND A SECOND RULING IN THE SAME STOP: `C_stat^data` needs its OWN TARGET FAMILY.** The background fluctuation lives in the measured target (`build_negweight_refined_target`), so reusing the 50 three-stream targets would leave the background Poisson inside the family **while `P3` asserts `bkg_bootstrap_factor_full` is unity — `P3` asserting something FALSE about the family, in the artifact, under a guard E wrote**, and carrying the largest MC contribution the product exists to exclude. `T1`–`T5` specified; **`build_fullevent_replica_target.py:215-222` AND `:205-213` both branch on the tag, never relax** — the second was not in the dispatch and fails too, because its closure targets the replica `1e6·R`. *(And `:205-213` is already a toleranced closure at a declared `rtol` — so `BEN-408`'s bit-exact-vs-toleranced split was a distinction this repo had ALREADY made, and `P5`'s defect was departing from it.)* [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-target-family-and-launchers.md) *Lane C (PET). | BEN-421 | **A VACUITY ARGUMENT IS ONLY AS COMPLETE AS ITS ENUMERATION OF DIFFERENCES — three of four limbs held and the fourth carried the entire experiment.** Proposed diagnostic: build one replica with **all three factors unity** and ask whether it reproduces the nominal. The mediator's own objection was that it may be **a null by construction** — *at unity the target is the nominal × a scalar, the MC is unthinned, `R` is nominal* — **which is `BEN-409`'s rule (a predicate that cannot fail has zero discriminating power) turned on a proposal rather than a guard.** **Three limbs verified TRUE:** `R` identical (unity data factor → `n_data_eff = n_data_rows`, `:945-951`); MC unthinned; **and `imc` identical**, because the replica driver reaches `nominal.main([...])` without `--estimator-seed`, so `subsample_seed = 0` from `NOMINAL_SEED_POLICY` gives the same 2,000,000-row subsample. **THE FOURTH IS FALSE: the nominal CONSUMES the archived Gate-2 target; a unity-factor replica REBUILDS it through the trained `u2d.refine_stay_positive`** (`fullevent_fps_dataloader.py:1462-1487`, *"Gate 5 requires a negweight-refined target built PER REPLICA"*), **so the two differ by the refit residual — measured, `0.068%` — plus one process's nondeterminism. Not by nothing.** **So the experiment asks whether a `0.068%` input perturbation produces the band's `186.1%` displacement (`nom/mean = 2.8610`): an amplification of `2.7 × 10³`. Not a check that cannot fail — a check whose failure has a NAME.** `0.068% → 186%` **IS** *"the estimator is honestly unstable at p‖ 6–20"*, i.e. **branch (a) measured directly rather than inferred**, and it is the only direct test of `(a)` anyone has proposed. **The inability to name a mechanism is evidence for vacuity only when the hypothesis space is closed; here the mechanism IS the hypothesis under test.** **AND A DISTINCTION I OWED AND HAD NOT DRAWN: `(a)`-AS-FACT WAS NEVER REFUTED — ONLY `(a)`-AS-DISPOSITION WAS.** Publishing bands that do not contain their own central value is incoherent whether or not the estimator is unstable, so my `OI-126` §2 killed the disposition and left the factual limb standing. **Conflating a refuted disposition with a refuted fact retires a measurable question by accident.** **ADMISSIBILITY, and it is NOT a third product:** `CSTAT-D4`, the reconciler's four distinctness labels and F7's *"one coherent Poisson factor per inventory member"* all assume a genuine draw, so a unity artifact is **never a family member** — **but `BEN-404`'s subject is a guard a new PRODUCT trips, and a product is what a covariance is built from. This builds none, so nothing trips.** Diagnostic, cost one target + one training, under four mechanical requirements: own tag (`diagnostic-unity-v1`, so `P1`/`T1` reject it), own root disjoint from both families (so `L2`'s tag⟺root rejects it), never seen by the reconciler, and the three-stream replay assertions **branch on the tag rather than skip**. **THRESHOLDS PRE-REGISTERED, including a VACUITY OUTCOME on my own non-vacuity claim** — band median `|ratio − 1|`: `≤ 5%` reproduces (a generous multiple of `VL130`'s `2.156%` top-occupancy floor, and the band is high-occupancy), `≥ 50%` does not, **`< 0.068%` means the experiment resolved less than its own input perturbation and was vacuous after all.** *A non-vacuity claim that cannot come out wrong is the same defect as the worry it answers.* **AND A THIRD FIGURE ASSEMBLED ACROSS RECEIPTS, WITHDRAWN: `4.6σ`.** The tracked single-source values are band `median z = +3.5546` (n=84) and `+3.8089` over the 63, with `44` above all 50; `family_mean/nominal` is `1/2.8610 = 0.3495`, **not the `0.246`** the `4.6σ` used, which I could not locate as a Gate-5 quantity and **did not guess at** — `0.2465231293324187` does occur in three Gate-6 files but as `per_member_dev/m5` and `absolute_deviation_from_one[2]`, and **a value match is evidence about the search, not the derivation** (`BEN-235`). **AND THE SHARPER HALF: a median of per-cell `z` over 84 CORRELATED cells IS NOT A σ.** So `3.6σ` is not the repair either — the honest form is *"the median band cell sits `3.55` family-sd above the family mean, and the nominal exceeds all fifty members in 44 of 84 cells"*, two statements and neither a significance. [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-unity-factor-diagnostic-is-admissible.md) *Lane C (PET), asked separately from Assistant with neither reading the other. **I did NOT authorize the ~3 A100-h** — the ruling says the experiment is sound and admissible, not that it should be bought; that is Joseph's, with its unit.* | `OI-126` | EP-2026-08-17-oi126-unity-diagnostic |
| BEN-422 | **DRAW-INDEPENDENCE REFUTES A VARIANCE MECHANISM AND SAYS NOTHING ABOUT A LOCATION FAILURE — the `OI-126` type mismatch, now inside the argument that RETIRED the route.** `FINDING-20260817-the-identity-claim-is-three-orders-too-strong.md` (`a17b8401`) establishes, correctly, that *"targets agree to `0.068%`"* was **the shared SCALAR, not a residual bound** — verified: `(1.000680923461914 − 1)×100 = 0.0680923461914` exactly — and that the measured per-row residual is median `0.0805%`, p90 `0.8286%`, p99 `6.888%`, **three orders above what an identity permits.** It then reports the residual **draw-independent** (`8.047e-04` at `data_factor == 1` vs `8.022e-04` at `≥ 2`) and concludes *"the `OI-126` target route is dead"*. **THAT INFERENCE DOES NOT FOLLOW. Draw-independence rules out a VARIANCE mechanism; the `OI-126` observation is a LOCATION failure — the NOMINAL sitting outside its own family, common-mode across all fifty replicas — and a draw-independent, common archived-vs-rebuilt offset is EXACTLY THE RIGHT SHAPE for that.** Nothing about the observation requires the offset to grow with the draw; the family's *width* was never what was anomalous. **This is the same dispersion-vs-location mismatch that made `{(a),(b)}` non-exhaustive, reappearing in the argument that retires a third route** — so the class survives being named, twice, in the same item. **AND THE CORRECTION STRENGTHENS THE EXPERIMENT IT WAS THOUGHT TO KILL.** Lane C's unity-factor ruling used `0.068%` as the residual, giving a required amplification of `2.7e3`; on the true residuals it is **`2313×` at the median and `27×` at p99.** **A `27×` amplification is enormously more plausible than `2700×`, so the input perturbation being bigger than stated makes the hypothesis CHEAPER to believe and the control MORE discriminating, not less.** Magnitudes marked superseded in place; the argument's structure unchanged. **THRESHOLDS REVISED BEFORE THE RESULT EXISTS, which is the only legitimate moment** (`BEN-403`): vacuity floor `< 0.068%` → **`< 0.0805%`**; `REPRODUCES ≤ 5%` stands (still `62×` the median input) **with a rider that is part of the threshold — the input's p99 is `6.888%`, so individual cells can exceed `5%` from the input alone, the statistic is the band MEDIAN, and per-cell excursions must not be read as failures.** **And the caveat that must not travel is the reason the thresholds are on a median at all:** the finding's `">1%: 8.456%"` tail is unconditioned with a near-zero-denominator pathology (max `686`) — **a tail fraction would inherit that; a median does not.** **TWO SMALLER CLOSURES.** (i) **`BEN-383`'s backend candidate dies on MECHANISM, not measurement:** `G2_GATE2_TARGET_RUNTIME_RECEIPT.json` carries `max_mc_events: 200000` and `refinement_random_state: 45`, **identical to the replica builder** (verified). **This refines my own `BEN-402`, which carried `BEN-383`'s phrase *"the two arms use different Stay-Positive backends"*: RIGHT about CONSUME-versus-REBUILD, WRONG if read as DIFFERENT-ALGORITHM. Anyone quoting `BEN-402` for a config difference is quoting the half that died.** (ii) **A re-priced object is the same run under a new description**, so *"a Gate-2 reproducibility control, not an `OI-126` experiment"* changes what the run is FOR and not what it MEASURES — though it does settle `BEN-404`'s third-product question from a second direction, since a control that reproduces an archived target builds no covariance. `U1`–`U4` stand, and `U2` matters more. [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-unity-factor-diagnostic-is-admissible.md) *Lane C (PET), re-ruling on the re-priced object at the mediator's request. | BEN-430 | **MATCH THE MOMENT, AND NEVER LET A NULL ON DEPENDENCE NAME A MECHANISM — two rules, three instances in one day, and each caught its own author on the very next inference.** **RULE 1 — MATCH THE MOMENT.** Before writing *"therefore"*, state which feature of the distribution the EVIDENCE constrains — location, dispersion, shape, or dependence-on-X — and which feature the CONCLUSION is about. **If they differ, the bridge is an argument that must be made, not an assumption.** *Instance: `{(a),(b)}` are both DISPERSION claims (honest instability, invalid proxy) while the `OI-126` observation — the nominal outside its own family — is a LOCATION fact. A location fact excludes neither dispersion claim and selects neither, which is exactly why the binary was never exhaustive.* **RULE 2 — A NULL ON DEPENDENCE EXCLUDES A FAMILY; IT DOES NOT NAME A MEMBER.** *"X does not depend on Y"* rules out **Y-driven** mechanisms and nothing else. **Before concluding *"therefore Z"*, enumerate the other Y-independent mechanisms and say why Z rather than them — and note the clause without which the rule reproduces the defect it fixes (lane C, `34ccd090`): the survivor list BOUNDS WHAT YOU MAY CLAIM and never certifies completeness. Two survivors does not mean the enumeration is unfinished, it means you cannot yet conclude; ONE survivor does not license concluding it, because the list is never provably complete. The first draft's *"if it has two you have not finished"* invited *enumerate until one survives, then conclude* — which would have licensed the very inference `BEN-422` refuted.** *Instance: draw-independence excludes draw-driven mechanisms — and nonlinear coupling AND a constant archived-vs-rebuilt construction offset BOTH survive it. The first was named without the second being listed, and `BEN-422` is the refutation.* **RULE 2 IS THE MECHANICAL ONE AND IT IS CHEAP: after any null, write the survivor list BEFORE the conclusion.** Here it would have had two entries and stopped the inference. **AND THE STRUCTURE THE THREE INSTANCES SHARE, which is the part worth keeping.** The dispersion-vs-location mismatch recurred *inside the argument retiring a third route, in a document written by the lane that had just filed the finding about it*; Assistant committed Rule 2's violation *on the very test it designed to be careful*; and this is the third time in one day after the find-the-authority rule and the allocation-by-suspicion rule. **In every case the rule was applied to OTHERS' claims and not to the author's own next inference. Naming a failure mode raises your detection rate on INPUTS and leaves your GENERATION rate unchanged.** So the durable form is not *"remember the rule"* — vigilance is not checkable — but **"apply the rule to your own conclusion in the same turn you apply it to someone else's"**, which is. [full](FINDING-20260817-match-the-moment-and-the-null-survivor-list.md) *Mediator lane, from Assistant's formulation; block `430-439` self-allocated, derived this turn: `grep -rhoE 'BEN-4(2[3-9]|3[0-9])'` over tracked and untracked returned 0, and `420-429` is lane C's. Filed against my own published error, not someone else's.* | `OI-126` | EP-2026-08-17-oi126-unity-diagnostic |
| BEN-431 | **A SLURM ARRAY TALLY NEEDS TWO COMMANDS, AND THE SUM IS THE CHECK.** `sacct -X` gives terminal states, `squeue -r` gives live ones, **and the two must sum to the declared array size; quoting either alone is a count of ROWS, not TASKS.** `sacct -X` suppresses `.batch`/`.extern` step rows but does **not** expand a pending `[12-49%10]`, which is one row under both. **`57194054` was reported to Joseph, lane C and lane E as `30 COMPLETED / 9 RUNNING / 1 PENDING`; it was `10 / 4 / 36`.** The sum test catches every variant without knowing the cause: the bad tally was `30+1+9 = 40 != 50`, and an `-X`-only read gives `10+4+1 = 15 != 50`. **AND THE FIRST DIAGNOSIS OF THE CAUSE WAS ITSELF WRONG, which is why this is filed rather than fixed in passing:** the mediator attributed it to misreading `-X` row counts — but `-X` was never used and `-X` alone returns the correct `10`. **The real cause: `--format=State%14` prints ONLY the State column, so `grep -vE '\.batch|\.extern'` had no JobID in the stream to match and removed nothing — a silent no-op filter, every task counted three times, both COMPLETED and RUNNING exactly `3x`.** **Fourth register of the check that cannot fire, after scope (`--diff-filter=D`, `BEN-391`), operating point (a ceiling at median `0.16`), and matching semantics (`200000` inside `2000000`): FIELD PRESENCE — a filter correctly written for a stream that does not exist.** *Number produced by lane Assistant, relayed by the mediator with no command run in the same turn (`BEN-027`) — producing a bad number and relaying one are different failures and both happened. It was also given as a clean tally with no derivation, where printing the command would have shown `40 != 50` at once: `BEN-077` applies to a one-line status number, not only to receipts. Bounded: the `~2 GPU-h` exposure estimate used the full array and did not depend on the tally, so the no-cancel decision is unchanged; only the timeline moved.* [full](FINDING-20260817-a-slurm-array-tally-needs-two-commands.md) | `OI-126` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-423 | **I SPECIFY PREDICATES BY NAMING WHAT THEY CONSULT RATHER THAN WHAT INDEPENDENCE THE COMPARISON HAS — and a predicate specified by LOCATION is satisfiable by a TAUTOLOGY at that location.** Third instance of one pattern in my own specifications, self-diagnosed. **INSTANCE 3 (the clean one):** my `R5` read *"the identity assertion consults `data_bootstrap_seed` AND the sha"*. Verified this turn: `train_fullevent_nominal.py:379` passes `precomputed_target=args.target_npy`; the loader echoes `os.path.abspath(precomputed_target)` to `consumed_precomputed_target` (`:1516`); `:233` computes `got_sha = sha256_file(target_npy)` from the same path. **So both provenance legs compare `X` to `X`, and the limb I had called LOAD-BEARING — the one converting intent into bytes — established NOTHING.** By my own standard (*a criterion satisfiable by whatever arrives is worth nothing*) `R5` was worthless as written. Found by lane B, verified by the mediator, verified again here. **INSTANCE 1:** `P5` named `hash_array(w_truth)` and the array was normalized in between, so it could not pass (`BEN-409`). **INSTANCE 2:** `BEN-430`'s Rule 2 named *enumerate the survivors* without bounding what a survivor list licenses, which I repaired — **same shape in someone else's rule, which is why I saw it there and not in mine.** **THE RULE: specify a provenance check by naming ITS TWO INDEPENDENT DERIVATIONS, never by naming the field it reads.** A field name is a **location**; independence is a **property**. `R5` amended to: *each leg compares a value the artifact ECHOES against a value derived by a route that does not pass through the echo's source* — admissible second operands are **family-position-derived** (`SEED_BASE + idx`, `campaign/replicas/replica_NN/target`), inadmissible is anything from the driver's own arguments. **AND THE WORKING FORM ALREADY EXISTED IN THE FILE THAT HAS TO BE FIXED ANYWAY**, which is the useful half: `validate_gate5_training_artifacts.py:283` derives `seed = SEED_BASE + idx` (`:145`) from the member's DIRECTORY POSITION, and `:285-287` derives `target_path` from `campaign/replicas/replica_NN/target`. **Both are genuinely independent routes. So `:283` is a GOOD check failing for a BAD reason — the overloaded FIELD, not the comparison — and it is repaired by pointing it at `data_bootstrap_seed`, never by weakening it.** **SECOND CONSUMER, AND THE EXPENSIVE ONE:** `:283` is a second identity-read of `bootstrap_seed` **downstream of the loader seam being patched**, so every data-only artifact would fail Gate-5 validation **at the end of the full training spend rather than at 2m24s.** Consequently `R2` (*`bootstrap_seed` read for identity NOWHERE on the data-only path*) is ruled to extend across **driver, loader-callers, target builder, extractor, validator and reconciler** — **because *"the data-only path"* is not a set of files, it is EVERY SITE THAT READS A FIELD THE DATA-ONLY ARTIFACT WRITES**, and the natural reading stops at the driver. Mechanical form: a covering `grep -rn 'bootstrap_seed'` over that stated corpus, classify every occurrence, pass only at **zero** identity-reads (`BEN-235` applied to my own criterion). [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-target-family-and-launchers.md) *Lane C (PET), self-diagnosis on a criterion I pre-registered one commit earlier. The pre-registration is what made the defect findable by someone else before the patch existed — a criterion written after the artifact could not have been caught this way.* | `OI-126` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-424 | **A DUPLICATED WRITER CANNOT DISAGREE WITH ITS ORIGINAL; A DUPLICATED READER CAN — so *"new file, never edit"* is NECESSARY AND INSUFFICIENT for a pinned READER, and the missing half is what makes divergence UNREPRESENTABLE rather than merely discouraged.** `R2`'s covering grep found four identity-reads of `bootstrap_seed` on the data-only path; two are in **PINNED** readers (`validate_gate5_training_artifacts.py:224,:283`, 3 digest sites; `reconcile_gate5_family.py:414/420/628`, 5 sites) where E probed that perturbing the file takes the gate red. **My launcher ruling (new unpinned files, never edits, never unified) transfers — but two launchers WRITING to disjoint roots cannot disagree, and two READERS can, so it buys a DUPLICATED VERDICT AUTHORITY whose failure mode is a data-only family verdicted PASS by a path whose divergence nobody must justify.** The mediator named that objection and it is correct. **RULED: WRAPPERS THAT IMPORT THE PINNED MODULES, never copies. A copy is a second implementation and diverges by default; a wrapper that delegates can only differ where it names a difference.** `V1` the wrapper contains **zero check logic** — every check executes byte-identically from the pinned original. `V2` a **DIVERGENCE MANIFEST as data**: each expected-to-fail check name carrying the exact `got`/`want` predicted, the data-only predicate it exists for, and its replacement assertion. `V3` the verdict is a **conjunction** — non-manifest checks PASSED, **manifest checks FAILED EXACTLY AS PREDICTED**, replacement assertions PASSED. `V4` a manifest **COUNT FLOOR** on the `RECEIPT_BINDING_FLOOR` model. **`V3`'s MIDDLE CLAUSE IS WHY THIS BEATS AN EDIT: an edit DELETES the check; the wrapper ASSERTS THE PINNED CHECK FAILED IN THE PREDICTED WAY.** A data-only artifact that unexpectedly PASSES the reclassified check, or fails it with a different `got`, **fails the wrapper. A reclassification is a pinned PREDICTION, not a blanket exemption — and no edit can be one, because an edit removes the observation the prediction is about.** So the objection dissolves rather than being mitigated: **there is no third state between *executed unchanged* and *named in a frozen manifest*.** **VERIFIED BEFORE RULING, because I prescribed a route from inspection once on this item and it was silently wrong** (`BEN-403(ii)`): both readers are importable (function-structured behind `__main__` guards); **both `Checks` classes record NAMED results with operands** (validator `:85-118`, reconciler `:237-262` — whose own docstring says *"every failure carries the two values that disagree, so the report can be contradicted by someone who never runs this tool"*, the exact affordance this consumes); and **zero `raise`/`SystemExit` in all four per-member functions**, so a data-only artifact yields a `Checks` object rather than a traceback. **That third precondition is the one that could have defeated the design and it is the one I checked hardest.** **THREE FORWARD PROHIBITIONS, the third new: never merge the wrapper into the original; never edit the original to accommodate it; and NEVER LET THE WRAPPER GROW A CHECK OF ITS OWN OUTSIDE THE MANIFEST — that is precisely how a wrapper becomes a second implementation, and it arrives one useful assertion at a time.** *(Unblocks the parked pairwise-distinctness diff as `V2` replacement assertions — and that exposure is DIRECTIONAL: duplicated targets bias `σ_stat^data` DOWN, the same direction as the frozen-`R` defect option (i) was refused for. Two independent routes to an understated `σ_stat` in a product whose purpose is external comparability; both must close.)* [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-pinned-readers-get-wrappers-not-copies.md) *Lane C (PET). Also adopts D's sharper rule, which strictly contains my `R5`: a check whose only route to failing is not the failure it claims to detect is not a measurement of that failure — independence is necessary, reachability-of-the-claimed-failure is what makes it sufficient.* | `OI-126`, `OI-60` | EP-2026-08-17-data-only-cstat |
| BEN-425 | **A GUARD THAT VALIDATES A VALUE MUST ENSURE THE VALUE IS PERSISTED, OR ITS CONCLUSION DOES NOT SURVIVE THE PROCESS — and a VERDICT PREDICATE MUST BOUND ITS DENOMINATOR, not only its numerator.** Two rules from one artifact, plus a withdrawal of my own inference. **(1) ASSERTED TWICE, RECORDED NOWHERE.** Two greps disagreed — `grep -c data_bootstrap_seed` in the FROZEN builder returned **2**, my repo read gave `:306`/`:299`/`:340` = **3** — and both were right about different objects. **`:294-298` builds a TRANSIENT DICT as the argument to `cdo.assert_data_only_target_streams(...)` and `:299` repeats the seed as a kwarg — NEITHER PERSISTS** (*"T1-T5 asserted over the block ABOUT TO BE WRITTEN"*); **`:301` is `write_npy(output, weights)`, so the target is a BARE `.npy` with no keys and cannot carry the field at all**; and **`:340`, the line `d14df112` added (8 comment + 1 code), is the RECEIPT write and the only persisting occurrence.** **So the built targets were VERIFIED to carry the right data-only seed and carry NO EVIDENCE OF IT — `BEN-245` at its sharpest: a guard validated a value into existence transiently and nothing wrote it down, so the guard's own conclusion is unfalsifiable afterwards.** `CONVENTION-receipt-ingredients` applied to a guard's **operands** rather than a report's numbers. **And it corrects my own `T4`, which said *"under its own key"* without saying WHERE: with a bare-`.npy` artifact the receipt is the only possible home, so `T4` is a RECEIPT predicate.** **(2) MY VERDICT PREDICATE COUNTED ONLY THE NUMERATOR.** `validate_gate5_training_artifacts.py:217` records the missing-key list, then `:218-219` **RETURNS EARLY** — **22 static check sites before it, 55 after.** So on an artifact missing one required key, **`V3`'s *"every non-manifest check PASSED"* is VACUOUSLY SATISFIED BY 55 UNEVALUATED CHECKS**, and `V4`'s floor was on **manifest size**, which the wrapper's own author writes: **a wrapper could go green having executed 22 of 77.** Found by lane E. **`V4` now floors on EXECUTED checks (`n_passed + n_failed`) — and one step further, because a floor is AUTHORED and can be set to 22: the executed count must EQUAL what `V5` observes on a coherent member, a number derived from the other path rather than written by the author. `V5` supplies `V4`'s operand and the two controls interlock** (`R5`'s independence-of-routes, applied to a count). **THIRD NUMERATOR I HAVE SPECIFIED TODAY** — `OI-94`'s data-only denominator, the sd-versus-variance share, this. Sibling of `BEN-423`, distinct axis. *(And the repo had already met this class one function away: `reconcile_gate5_family.py:867-868`'s `invariant_path_resolves`, noted *"absent from every replica: the check below would pass vacuously"* — so the fix has a local idiom to copy rather than invent.)* **(3) MY PRECONDITION CHECK COULD NOT HAVE SEEN THE DEFEAT.** I certified *"no per-member function raises"* by `grep -c 'raise\|SystemExit'`. Measured by E: **missing key → `KeyError` from numpy; present-but-`None` → `TypeError` from `int()` at `:224`. Neither is a `raise`.** `BEN-235` on my own precondition. **Restated: no function raises, AND every pinned check's operands are PRESENT and TYPED AS THE PINNED CODE COERCES THEM** — the second clause carries the weight and I did not have it. **(4) WITHDRAWN — my inbound-`BLOCK`-on-`code.target_builder` claim.** `57194054` executes the frozen deployment `d0c42bdd` (clean tree), so `d14df112` changed nothing it uses. **I checked when the REPO's builder changed and inferred what the RUNNING ARRAY uses** — `BEN-403(ii)` on the one axis I could not check from this host, which is exactly when an inference must be labelled as one. **The frozen-deployment discipline worked and I read past it.** *(The METHOD rule survives its instance: before pricing a fix against artifacts you would rebuild, check whether they still satisfy their own family invariants — against the EXECUTING tree.)* [full](RULING-20260817-lanec-data-only-target-family-and-launchers.md) *Lane C (PET).
